=== FILE: src/components/PillCapsule.tsx ===
import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import {
  Check,
  Coffee,
  Download,
  Eye,
  EyeOff,
  FolderDown,
  Mic,
  MicOff,
  Phone,
  PhoneOff,
  ShieldCheck,
  Sparkles,
  Sun,
  Terminal,
  Timer,
  Volume2,
  Zap,
} from 'lucide-react';
import {
  AgentApprovalRequest,
  AppearanceMaterial,
  AudioOutputDevice,
  ClipboardItem,
  EdgeLightProfile,
  IncomingCallSession,
  PillPreferences,
  PillTab,
  MusicTrack,
  ReminderTask,
  TransientHudKind,
  TrayFileItem,
  VoiceAgentAction,
  WindowsSystemState,
} from '../types/pill';
import { formatDurationClock, playTactilePop } from '../utils/audioSynth';
import { computeBatteryEta } from '../utils/customizationLogic';
import {
  computeLiquidDeformation,
  isSpringAtRest,
  PillAnchorPosition,
  PillShapeStyle,
  resolvePillGeometry,
  SpringState1D,
  stepSpring1D,
} from '../utils/springPhysics';
import {
  getThemeIconUrl,
  getThemeMotifUrl,
  resolveEdgeGlowCss,
  resolveThemePalette,
} from '../utils/themeAndSliders';
import { AnimatedBatterySymbol } from './AnimatedBatterySymbol';
import { FluidEffectSlider } from './FluidEffectSlider';
import { ThemedIcon } from './ThemedIcon';
import { FluidMusicVisualizer } from './FluidMusicVisualizer';
import { PillExpandedContent } from './PillExpandedContent';
import { ResilientThumb } from './ResilientThumb';
import { InPillAstraPanel } from './VoiceAgentOverlay';

export interface PillCapsuleProps {
  isExpanded: boolean;
  onExpand: (tab?: PillTab) => void;
  onCollapse: () => void;
  activeTab: PillTab;
  onSelectTab: (tab: PillTab) => void;
  isLauncherOpen: boolean;
  onToggleLauncher: () => void;
  onOpenFullSettings: () => void;
  isAstraOpen: boolean;
  onOpenVoiceAgent: () => void;
  onCloseVoiceAgent: () => void;
  onExecuteAgentActions: (actions: VoiceAgentAction[]) => void;

  // Media
  currentTrack: MusicTrack;
  isPlaying: boolean;
  trackProgressSec: number;
  onTogglePlay: () => void;
  onPrevTrack: () => void;
  onNextTrack: () => void;
  onSeekTrack: (sec: number) => void;
  audioDevice: AudioOutputDevice;
  onChangeAudioDevice: (dev: AudioOutputDevice) => void;

  // Secondary Live Activity (Timer / Keep Awake)
  timerRemainingSec: number | null;
  timerTotalSec: number;
  onStartTimer: (sec: number) => void;
  onCancelTimer: () => void;

  // System & Control Center
  systemState: WindowsSystemState;
  onUpdateSystemState: (patch: Partial<WindowsSystemState>) => void;

  // Transient HUDs
  transientHud: TransientHudKind;
  volumeLevel: number;
  onChangeVolume: (val: number) => void;
  brightnessLevel: number;
  onChangeBrightness: (val: number) => void;
  callSession: IncomingCallSession | null;
  onAcceptCall: () => void;
  onToggleCallMute: () => void;
  onEndCall: () => void;
  agentRequest: AgentApprovalRequest | null;
  onResolveAgentRequest: (approved: boolean) => void;

  // Quick Toggles
  keepAwake: boolean;
  onToggleKeepAwake: () => void;
  micMuted: boolean;
  onToggleMicMuted: () => void;
  nightLight: boolean;
  onToggleNightLight: () => void;
  onTriggerQuickActionToast: (msg: string) => void;

  // Tray, Clipboard, Notes, Reminders
  trayFiles: TrayFileItem[];
  onAddTrayFiles: (files: TrayFileItem[]) => void;
  onTogglePinTrayFile: (id: string) => void;
  onRemoveTrayFile: (id: string) => void;
  onClearUnpinnedTray: () => void;
  onRestoreSampleTray: () => void;

  clipboardItems: ClipboardItem[];
  onAddClipboardItem: (item: Omit<ClipboardItem, 'id' | 'copiedAt' | 'pinned'>) => void;
  onTogglePinClip: (id: string) => void;
  onRemoveClipboardItem: (id: string) => void;
  onClearUnpinnedClips: () => void;

  noteContent: string;
  onChangeNoteContent: (val: string) => void;

  reminders: ReminderTask[];
  onToggleReminder: (id: string) => void;
  onAddReminder: (title: string) => void;

  preferences: PillPreferences;
  onUpdatePreferences: (patch: Partial<PillPreferences>) => void;
  stealthRevealedByShortcut: boolean;
}

export const PillCapsule: React.FC<PillCapsuleProps> = ({
  isExpanded,
  onExpand,
  onCollapse,
  activeTab,
  onSelectTab,
  isLauncherOpen,
  onToggleLauncher,
  onOpenFullSettings,
  isAstraOpen,
  onOpenVoiceAgent,
  onCloseVoiceAgent,
  onExecuteAgentActions,
  currentTrack,
  isPlaying,
  trackProgressSec,
  onTogglePlay,
  onPrevTrack,
  onNextTrack,
  onSeekTrack,
  audioDevice,
  onChangeAudioDevice,
  timerRemainingSec,
  timerTotalSec,
  onStartTimer,
  onCancelTimer,
  systemState,
  onUpdateSystemState,
  transientHud,
  volumeLevel,
  onChangeVolume,
  brightnessLevel,
  onChangeBrightness,
  callSession,
  onAcceptCall,
  onToggleCallMute,
  onEndCall,
  agentRequest,
  onResolveAgentRequest,
  keepAwake,
  onToggleKeepAwake,
  micMuted,
  onToggleMicMuted,
  nightLight,
  onToggleNightLight,
  onTriggerQuickActionToast,
  trayFiles,
  onAddTrayFiles,
  onTogglePinTrayFile,
  onRemoveTrayFile,
  onClearUnpinnedTray,
  onRestoreSampleTray,
  clipboardItems,
  onAddClipboardItem,
  onTogglePinClip,
  onRemoveClipboardItem,
  onClearUnpinnedClips,
  noteContent,
  onChangeNoteContent,
  reminders,
  onToggleReminder,
  onAddReminder,
  preferences,
  onUpdatePreferences,
  stealthRevealedByShortcut,
}) => {
  const [isHovered, setIsHovered] = useState(false);
  const [edgeTouched, setEdgeTouched] = useState(false);
  const [isDragOver, setIsDragOver] = useState(false);
  const [astraHasResult, setAstraHasResult] = useState(false);
  const [contextMenuPos, setContextMenuPos] = useState<{ x: number; y: number } | null>(null);

  const isExpandedRef = useRef(isExpanded);
  isExpandedRef.current = isExpanded;

  const palette = resolveThemePalette(
    preferences.themeId,
    preferences.customPrimaryHex,
    preferences.customSecondaryHex
  );

  const effectiveAstraActive = Boolean(isAstraOpen && preferences.aiEnabled && !isExpanded);

  const musicVisible = preferences.featureFlags.nowPlaying && Boolean(currentTrack);
  const timerVisible = preferences.featureFlags.quickTimers && timerRemainingSec !== null;

  const hasPrimaryActivity =
    musicVisible ||
    timerVisible ||
    preferences.showCompactStats ||
    keepAwake;

  const hasSecondaryDroplet =
    !isExpanded &&
    !effectiveAstraActive &&
    transientHud === 'none' &&
    (preferences.shapeStyle === PillShapeStyle.SplitDualCapsule || (musicVisible && (timerVisible || keepAwake)));

  const isStealthHidden =
    preferences.stealthAutoHide &&
    !isExpanded &&
    !effectiveAstraActive &&
    transientHud === 'none' &&
    !isHovered &&
    !edgeTouched &&
    !isDragOver &&
    !stealthRevealedByShortcut;

  const targetGeometry = useMemo(
    () =>
      resolvePillGeometry({
        isExpanded,
        isLauncherOpen,
        activeTab,
        transientKind: transientHud,
        hasPrimaryActivity,
        hasSecondaryDroplet,
        isHovered,
        isDragOver,
        sizeScale: preferences.sizeScale,
        isElongated: preferences.isElongated,
        shapeStyle: preferences.shapeStyle,
        anchorPosition: preferences.anchorPosition,
        floatingOffsetPx: preferences.floatingOffsetPx,
        isStealthHidden,
        isAstraActive: effectiveAstraActive,
        astraMode: preferences.astraMode,
        astraExpandedResult: astraHasResult,
      }),
    [
      isExpanded,
      isLauncherOpen,
      activeTab,
      transientHud,
      hasPrimaryActivity,
      hasSecondaryDroplet,
      isHovered,
      isDragOver,
      preferences.sizeScale,
      preferences.isElongated,
      preferences.shapeStyle,
      preferences.anchorPosition,
      preferences.floatingOffsetPx,
      isStealthHidden,
      effectiveAstraActive,
      preferences.astraMode,
      astraHasResult,
    ]
  );

  // Refs for 0%-idle-CPU DirectComposition-equivalent rAF spring loop
  const wrapperRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);
  const collapseTimerRef = useRef<number | null>(null);
  const lastHitRect = useRef<{ x: number; y: number; width: number; height: number }>({
    x: 0,
    y: 0,
    width: 0,
    height: 0,
  });
  const mainSurfaceRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);
  const mainGooPillRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);
  const dropletGooPillRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);
  const dropletInteractiveRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement | null>(null);

  const preferencesRef = useRef(preferences);
  preferencesRef.current = preferences;

  const contextMenuPosRef = useRef(contextMenuPos);
  contextMenuPosRef.current = contextMenuPos;

  const targetGeoRef = useRef(targetGeometry);
  targetGeoRef.current = targetGeometry;

  const springConfigRef = useRef(preferences.customSpring);
  springConfigRef.current = preferences.customSpring;

  const anchorRef = useRef(preferences.anchorPosition);
  anchorRef.current = preferences.anchorPosition;

  const physicsStateRef = useRef<{
    w: SpringState1D;
    h: SpringState1D;
    r: SpringState1D;
    top: SpringState1D;
    satOffset: SpringState1D;
    satScale: SpringState1D;
  }>({
    w: { position: targetGeometry.width, velocity: 0 },
    h: { position: targetGeometry.height, velocity: 0 },
    r: { position: targetGeometry.borderRadius, velocity: 0 },
    top: { position: targetGeometry.topOffset, velocity: 0 },
    satOffset: {
      position:
        targetGeometry.satelliteSize > 0
          ? targetGeometry.satelliteGap + targetGeometry.satelliteSize
          : -28,
      velocity: 0,
    },
    satScale: { position: targetGeometry.satelliteSize > 0 ? 1 : 0.2, velocity: 0 },
  });

  const kickLoopRef = useRef<(() => void) | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    let rafId = 0;
    let lastTime = performance.now();

    const applyFrame = (now: number) => {
      const dt = Math.min(0.05, Math.max(0.001, (now - lastTime) / 1000));
      lastTime = now;

      const target = targetGeoRef.current;
      const cfg = springConfigRef.current;
      const s = physicsStateRef.current;
      const isBottom = anchorRef.current === PillAnchorPosition.BottomTaskbar;

      const targetSatOffset =
        target.satelliteSize > 0 ? target.satelliteGap + target.satelliteSize : -26;
      const targetSatScale = target.satelliteSize > 0 ? 1 : 0.15;

      s.w = stepSpring1D(s.w, target.width, cfg, dt);
      s.h = stepSpring1D(s.h, target.height, cfg, dt);
      s.r = stepSpring1D(s.r, target.borderRadius, cfg, dt);
      s.top = stepSpring1D(s.top, target.topOffset, cfg, dt);
      s.satOffset = stepSpring1D(s.satOffset, targetSatOffset, cfg, dt);
      s.satScale = stepSpring1D(s.satScale, targetSatScale, cfg, dt);

      const deform = computeLiquidDeformation(s.w.velocity, s.h.velocity);

      const wPx = Math.max(80, s.w.position);
      const hPx = Math.max(6, s.h.position);
      const rPx = Math.max(6, s.r.position);
      const offsetPx = s.top.position;

      if (wrapperRef.current) {
        const yTranslate = isBottom ? -offsetPx : offsetPx;
        wrapperRef.current.style.transform = `translate3d(0, ${yTranslate.toFixed(2)}px, 0)`;

        if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && '__TAURI_INTERNALS__' in window) {
          const r = wrapperRef.current.getBoundingClientRect();
          const extraH = contextMenuPosRef.current ? 290 : 0;
          if (
            Math.abs(r.x - lastHitRect.current.x) > 1 ||
            Math.abs(r.y - lastHitRect.current.y) > 1 ||
            Math.abs(r.width - lastHitRect.current.width) > 1 ||
            Math.abs(r.height + extraH - lastHitRect.current.height) > 1
          ) {
            lastHitRect.current = { x: r.x, y: r.y, width: r.width, height: r.height + extraH };
            import('@tauri-apps/api/core')
              .then(({ invoke }) => {
                invoke('update_pill_hit_rect', { rect: lastHitRect.current }).catch(() => {});
              })
              .catch(() => {});
          }
        }
      }

      // Enforce true 360-degree semicircular pill curvature when compact (NEVER square)
      // Clamped strictly to target.borderRadius when expanded (NEVER an oval/egg abomination)
      let effectiveRadius = rPx;
      const currentShape = preferencesRef.current.shapeStyle;
      if (!isExpandedRef.current && hPx <= 60) {
        if (
          currentShape === PillShapeStyle.DynamicPill ||
          currentShape === PillShapeStyle.SplitDualCapsule ||
          currentShape === PillShapeStyle.TopBezelPill
        ) {
          effectiveRadius = Math.max(effectiveRadius, Math.round(hPx / 2));
        }
      } else {
        effectiveRadius = Math.min(rPx, target.borderRadius || 34);
      }

      const borderBottomRadius = `${effectiveRadius.toFixed(1)}px`;
      const borderTopRadius = target.isDockedFlushTop ? '0px' : `${effectiveRadius.toFixed(1)}px`;
      const fullRadius = `${borderTopRadius} ${borderTopRadius} ${borderBottomRadius} ${borderBottomRadius}`;

      if (mainSurfaceRef.current) {
        mainSurfaceRef.current.style.width = `${wPx.toFixed(1)}px`;
        mainSurfaceRef.current.style.height = `${hPx.toFixed(1)}px`;
        mainSurfaceRef.current.style.borderRadius = fullRadius;
      }

      if (mainGooPillRef.current) {
        mainGooPillRef.current.style.width = `${wPx.toFixed(1)}px`;
        mainGooPillRef.current.style.height = `${hPx.toFixed(1)}px`;
        mainGooPillRef.current.style.borderRadius = fullRadius;
      }

      const satSize = Math.max(24, target.satelliteSize || 36);
      const satX = s.satOffset.position;
      const satS = Math.max(0.01, s.satScale.position);

      if (dropletGooPillRef.current) {
        dropletGooPillRef.current.style.width = `${satSize}px`;
        dropletGooPillRef.current.style.height = `${satSize}px`;
        dropletGooPillRef.current.style.transform = `translate3d(${satX.toFixed(1)}px, 0, 0) scale(${satS.toFixed(3)})`;
        dropletGooPillRef.current.style.opacity = satS > 0.18 ? '1' : '0';
      }

      if (dropletInteractiveRef.current) {
        dropletInteractiveRef.current.style.width = `${satSize}px`;
        dropletInteractiveRef.current.style.height = `${satSize}px`;
        dropletInteractiveRef.current.style.transform = `translate3d(${satX.toFixed(1)}px, 0, 0) scale(${satS.toFixed(3)})`;
        dropletInteractiveRef.current.style.opacity = satS > 0.55 ? '1' : '0';
        dropletInteractiveRef.current.style.pointerEvents = satS > 0.7 ? 'auto' : 'none';
      }

      const allSettled =
        isSpringAtRest(s.w, target.width) &&
        isSpringAtRest(s.h, target.height) &&
        isSpringAtRest(s.r, target.borderRadius) &&
        isSpringAtRest(s.top, target.topOffset) &&
        isSpringAtRest(s.satOffset, targetSatOffset) &&
        isSpringAtRest(s.satScale, targetSatScale);

      if (!allSettled) {
        rafId = requestAnimationFrame(applyFrame);
      } else {
        rafId = 0;
      }
    };

    const kick = () => {
      if (rafId === 0) {
        lastTime = performance.now();
        rafId = requestAnimationFrame(applyFrame);
      }
    };

    kickLoopRef.current = kick;
    kick();

    return () => {
      if (rafId !== 0) cancelAnimationFrame(rafId);
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    kickLoopRef.current?.();
  }, [targetGeometry, preferences.customSpring, preferences.anchorPosition]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && '__TAURI_INTERNALS__' in window) {
      import('@tauri-apps/api/core').then(({ invoke }) => {
        invoke('reposition_pill_window', { anchor: preferences.anchorPosition }).catch(() => {});
      }).catch(() => {});
    }
  }, [preferences.anchorPosition]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const onKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') {
        setContextMenuPos(null);
        if (effectiveAstraActive) onCloseVoiceAgent();
        else if (isExpanded) onCollapse();
      }
    };
    const onWindowClick = () => setContextMenuPos(null);
    const onWindowBlur = () => setContextMenuPos(null);
    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    window.addEventListener('click', onWindowClick);
    window.addEventListener('blur', onWindowBlur);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
      window.removeEventListener('click', onWindowClick);
      window.removeEventListener('blur', onWindowBlur);
    };
  }, [isExpanded, effectiveAstraActive, onCollapse, onCloseVoiceAgent]);

  // Synchronize Tauri OS window pass-through hit testing when context menu opens/closes
  useEffect(() => {
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && '__TAURI_INTERNALS__' in window) {
      import('@tauri-apps/api/core')
        .then(({ invoke }) => {
          invoke('set_pill_force_interactive', { force: Boolean(contextMenuPos) }).catch(() => {});
        })
        .catch(() => {});
    }
    kickLoopRef.current?.();
    return () => {
      if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && '__TAURI_INTERNALS__' in window) {
        import('@tauri-apps/api/core')
          .then(({ invoke }) => {
            invoke('set_pill_force_interactive', { force: false }).catch(() => {});
          })
          .catch(() => {});
      }
    };
  }, [contextMenuPos]);

  // Listen to native OS cursor boundary events (120Hz precision from window_manager.rs)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (typeof window === 'undefined' || !('__TAURI_INTERNALS__' in window)) return;
    let unlistenLeft: (() => void) | null = null;
    let unlistenEntered: (() => void) | null = null;
    let unlistenOutside: (() => void) | null = null;
    let leaveToleranceTimer: number | null = null;

    import('@tauri-apps/api/event').then(({ listen }) => {
      listen('pill://cursor-left-pill', () => {
        if (leaveToleranceTimer !== null) window.clearTimeout(leaveToleranceTimer);
        leaveToleranceTimer = window.setTimeout(() => {
          leaveToleranceTimer = null;
          setIsHovered(false);
          setEdgeTouched(false);
        }, 450);
      }).then((u) => {
        unlistenLeft = u;
      });

      listen('pill://cursor-entered-pill', () => {
        if (leaveToleranceTimer !== null) {
          window.clearTimeout(leaveToleranceTimer);
          leaveToleranceTimer = null;
        }
        setIsHovered(true);
      }).then((u) => {
        unlistenEntered = u;
      });

      listen('pill://outside-click', () => {
        if (leaveToleranceTimer !== null) {
          window.clearTimeout(leaveToleranceTimer);
          leaveToleranceTimer = null;
        }
        setIsHovered(false);
        setEdgeTouched(false);
        setContextMenuPos(null);
      }).then((u) => {
        unlistenOutside = u;
      });
    });

    return () => {
      if (leaveToleranceTimer !== null) window.clearTimeout(leaveToleranceTimer);
      unlistenLeft?.();
      unlistenEntered?.();
      unlistenOutside?.();
    };
  }, []);

  const handleDragOver = (e: React.DragEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!isDragOver) setIsDragOver(true);
  };

  const handleDragLeave = (e: React.DragEvent) => {
    if (!e.currentTarget.contains(e.relatedTarget as Node)) {
      setIsDragOver(false);
    }
  };

  const handleDrop = (e: React.DragEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setIsDragOver(false);

    const customDesktopPayload =
      e.dataTransfer.getData('application/x-dynamic-pill-file') ||
      e.dataTransfer.getData('application/x-dynamic-ledge-file');
    if (customDesktopPayload) {
      try {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(customDesktopPayload) as TrayFileItem;
        onAddTrayFiles([{ ...parsed, id: `drop-${Date.now()}`, addedAt: 'Just now' }]);
        playTactilePop('drop');
        onExpand('tray');
        return;
      } catch {
        // Ignore
      }
    }

    if (e.dataTransfer.files && e.dataTransfer.files.length > 0) {
      const dropped: TrayFileItem[] = Array.from(e.dataTransfer.files).map((f, idx) => {
        const ext = f.name.includes('.')
          ? f.name.split('.').pop()!.toUpperCase().slice(0, 5)
          : 'FILE';
        const kb = Math.max(0.5, f.size / 1024);
        return {
          id: `os-drop-${Date.now()}-${idx}`,
          name: f.name,
          extension: ext,
          sizeLabel: kb >= 1024 ? `${(kb / 1024).toFixed(1)} MB` : `${kb.toFixed(1)} KB`,
          addedAt: 'Just now',
          pinned: false,
          contentPreview: `Dropped onto Dynamic Pill: ${f.name}`,
        };
      });
      onAddTrayFiles(dropped);
      playTactilePop('drop');
      onExpand('tray');
    }
  };

  const isLightPaper = Boolean(palette.isLightSurface && !effectiveAstraActive);
  const isGlass = !isLightPaper && preferences.material === AppearanceMaterial.LiquidGlass;
  const isGraphite = !isLightPaper && preferences.material === AppearanceMaterial.FrostedGraphite;
  const isDockedBezel = targetGeometry.isDockedFlushTop;
  const isBottomAnchor = preferences.anchorPosition === PillAnchorPosition.BottomTaskbar;
  const isPureOrbOnly = Boolean(effectiveAstraActive && preferences.astraMode === 'orb_only' && !astraHasResult);

  // Compute Edge Lighting Aura (or Siri Rainbow Aura when Astra is morphed in-Pill)
  const shouldShowEdgeLight =
    preferences.featureFlags.edgeLighting &&
    !preferences.featureFlags.lowRamEcoMode &&
    preferences.edgeLightProfile !== 'off' &&
    (preferences.edgeLightTrigger === 'always' ||
      (preferences.edgeLightTrigger === 'music_only' && isPlaying) ||
      (preferences.edgeLightTrigger === 'alerts_only' && transientHud !== 'none'));

  const edgeShadowCss = effectiveAstraActive
    ? '0 -2px 24px rgba(56,189,248,0.52), -8px 6px 34px rgba(168,85,247,0.42), 8px 6px 34px rgba(244,63,165,0.42)'
    : shouldShowEdgeLight
      ? resolveEdgeGlowCss(
          preferences.edgeLightProfile,
          preferences.edgeLightIntensity,
          palette.primaryHex,
          palette.secondaryHex
        )
      : undefined;

  const anchorFlexClass =
    preferences.anchorPosition === PillAnchorPosition.TopLeft
      ? 'justify-start pl-8'
      : preferences.anchorPosition === PillAnchorPosition.TopRight
        ? 'justify-end pr-8'
        : 'justify-center';

  const batteryEta = computeBatteryEta(
    systemState.batteryPct,
    systemState.isCharging,
    systemState.chargingWatts
  );

  // OS-Level Privacy Masking (`SetWindowDisplayAffinity(hwnd, WDA_EXCLUDEFROMCAPTURE)`):
  // Determine whether remote screen-share callers are blocked from seeing the current Pill state.
  const isPublicPassthroughAllowed =
    preferences.privacyPublicPassthroughHud &&
    !isExpanded &&
    !effectiveAstraActive &&
    transientHud === 'none' &&
    (musicVisible || timerVisible);

  const isCloakedFromScreenCapture =
    preferences.privacyMaskEnabled &&
    preferences.privacySimulateCaptureView &&
    !isPublicPassthroughAllowed;

  return (
    <>
      {/* Caller Screen-Share Simulator Banner when previewing WDA_EXCLUDEFROMCAPTURE */}
      {preferences.privacySimulateCaptureView && (
        <div
          onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
          className="fixed top-16 right-4 z-50 flex items-center gap-2.5 px-3.5 py-2 rounded-2xl bg-emerald-950/92 backdrop-blur-xl border border-emerald-400/35 shadow-2xl text-xs text-emerald-100"
        >
          <EyeOff className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-300 shrink-0" />
          <div>
            <div className="font-semibold text-white">
              Caller Screen-Share View ({preferences.privacyMaskEnabled ? 'Mask Active' : 'Unmasked'})
            </div>
            <div className="text-[10.5px] text-emerald-200/85">
              {isCloakedFromScreenCapture
                ? 'Zoom/Teams/Meet viewers only see your desktop â€” Pill is excluded from capture'
                : preferences.privacyMaskEnabled
                  ? 'Public Now Playing HUD visible to callers Â· Assist/Notes/Clipboard remain hidden'
                  : 'Privacy Mask is Off â€” callers can see Pill'}
            </div>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"

                    />

                      CPU {systemState.cpuUsagePct.toFixed(0)}%
                    </span>
                  </>
                ) : keepAwake ? (
                  <>
                    <span className="text-[11px] font-medium text-emerald-300">Awake</span>
                    <span
                      aria-hidden="true"
                      className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-[#0c1018] ring-1 ring-white/10"
                    />
                    <Coffee className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-300" />
                  </>
                ) : (
                  <div className="w-full h-full flex items-center justify-between px-3 select-none">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0">
                      <img
                        src={getThemeIconUrl(preferences.themeId)}
                        alt="The Pill"
                        className="w-5 h-5 rounded-md object-contain shrink-0 ring-1 ring-white/20"
                      />
                      <span
                        className={`text-xs font-semibold truncate ${
                          isLightPaper ? 'text-stone-900' : 'text-slate-100'
                        }`}
                      >
                        {palette.name}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
                      <span
                        aria-hidden="true"
                        className={`w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full ${
                          isLightPaper
                            ? 'bg-stone-800/80 ring-1 ring-stone-900/20'
                            : 'bg-[#0c1018] ring-1 ring-white/10'
                        }`}
                      />
                      <span
                        className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full animate-pulse"
                        style={{ backgroundColor: palette.primaryHex }}
                      />
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}
              </button>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Layer 3: Detached Secondary Live-Activity Satellite Droplet */}
          <button
            ref={dropletInteractiveRef}
            type="button"
            onClick={(e) => {
              e.stopPropagation();
              playTactilePop('expand');
              onExpand('home');
            }}
            aria-label={
              timerRemainingSec !== null
                ? `Active timer ${formatDurationClock(timerRemainingSec)} â€” click to open`
                : 'Secondary island status â€” click to open'
            }
            style={{
              right: 0,
              top: 0,
            }}
            className={`absolute right-0 top-0 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-sky-400 ${
              isCloakedFromScreenCapture ? 'invisible' : ''
            } ${isLightPaper ? 'ring-1 ring-stone-900/20' : 'ring-1 ring-white/14'}`}
          >
            {timerRemainingSec !== null ? (
              <span
                className={`text-[10px] font-mono font-semibold tabular-nums ${
                  isLightPaper ? 'text-amber-800' : 'text-amber-300'
                }`}
              >
                {timerRemainingSec >= 60
                  ? `${Math.ceil(timerRemainingSec / 60)}m`
                  : `${timerRemainingSec}s`}
              </span>
            ) : keepAwake ? (
              <Coffee
                className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 ${isLightPaper ? 'text-emerald-800' : 'text-emerald-300'}`}
              />
            ) : (
              <Zap
                className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 ${isLightPaper ? 'text-amber-800' : 'text-sky-300'}`}
              />
            )}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Right-Click Quick Context Menu */}
      {contextMenuPos && (
        <>
          <div
            className="fixed inset-0 z-40 bg-transparent pointer-events-auto cursor-default"
            onClick={(e) => {
              e.preventDefault();
              e.stopPropagation();
              setContextMenuPos(null);
              playTactilePop('collapse');
            }}
            onContextMenu={(e) => {
              e.preventDefault();
              e.stopPropagation();
              setContextMenuPos(null);
            }}
          />
          <div
            role="menu"
            aria-label="Dynamic Island quick controls"
            style={{
              left: `${Math.min(Math.max(10, contextMenuPos.x), (typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window.innerWidth : 960) - 250)}px`,
              top: `${Math.min(Math.max(10, contextMenuPos.y), (typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window.innerHeight : 600) - 310)}px`,
            }}
            onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
            onContextMenu={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
            className="fixed z-50 w-60 bg-slate-950/95 backdrop-blur-xl border border-white/15 rounded-xl p-1.5 shadow-2xl text-xs text-slate-200 space-y-0.5 pointer-events-auto select-none"
          >
            <button
              type="button"
              role="menuitem"
              onClick={() => {
                setContextMenuPos(null);
                playTactilePop('confirm');
                if (isExpanded) onCollapse();
                else onExpand('home');
              }}
              className="w-full text-left px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-white/10 active:bg-white/15 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
            >
              {isExpanded ? 'Collapse Island' : 'Expand Home Hub'}
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              role="menuitem"
              onClick={() => {
                setContextMenuPos(null);
                playTactilePop('confirm');
                onExpand('control');
              }}
              className="w-full text-left px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-white/10 active:bg-white/15 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
            >
              Open Windows Control Center
            </button>
            {preferences.aiEnabled && (
              <button
                type="button"
                role="menuitem"
                onClick={() => {
                  setContextMenuPos(null);
                  playTactilePop('confirm');
                  if (effectiveAstraActive) onCloseVoiceAgent();
                  else onOpenVoiceAgent();
                }}
                className="w-full flex items-center justify-between px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-white/10 active:bg-white/15 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
              >
                <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <Sparkles className="w-3.5 h-3.5" style={{ color: palette.primaryHex }} />
                  <span>{effectiveAstraActive ? 'Exit Astra Pill Mode' : 'Morph Pill into Astra'}</span>
                </span>
                <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-slate-400">
                  {preferences.voiceAgentShortcut}
                </span>
              </button>
            )}
            <button
              type="button"
              role="menuitem"
              onClick={() => {
                setContextMenuPos(null);
                playTactilePop('confirm');
                onUpdatePreferences({ privacyMaskEnabled: !preferences.privacyMaskEnabled });
              }}
              className="w-full flex items-center justify-between px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-white/10 active:bg-white/15 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
            >
              <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <ShieldCheck className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-400" />
                <span>OS Privacy Mask</span>
              </span>
              <span className="text-emerald-300 font-mono text-[11px]">
                {preferences.privacyMaskEnabled ? 'On' : 'Off'}
              </span>
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              role="menuitem"
              onClick={() => {
                setContextMenuPos(null);
                playTactilePop('confirm');
                onUpdatePreferences({
                  privacySimulateCaptureView: !preferences.privacySimulateCaptureView,
                });
              }}
              className="w-full flex items-center justify-between px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-white/10 active:bg-white/15 transition-colors cursor-pointer"

                ? 'Top-Bezel Pill Dock'
                : 'Dynamic Island Pill'}

              type="button"
              role="menuitem"
              onClick={() => {

                onUpdatePreferences({ stealthAutoHide: !preferences.stealthAutoHide });
              }}
              className="w-full flex items-center justify-between px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-white/10 active:bg-white/15 transition-colors cursor-pointer"

                setContextMenuPos(null);
                playTactilePop('confirm');

            >
              Full Studio Settingsâ€¦
            </button>
          </div>
        </>
      )}
    </>
  );
};

type ClipFilterGroup = 'all' | 'text_code' | 'media' | 'files' | 'colors_links';

function isBuiltinTab(tab: PillTab): tab is BuiltinPillTab {
  return !tab.startsWith('custom_');
}

function resolveGridSpanClass(size: WidgetSizeVariant): string {
  switch (size) {
    case 'small':
      return 'col-span-2 sm:col-span-1 row-span-1';
    case 'medium':
      return 'col-span-4 sm:col-span-2 row-span-1';
    case 'large':
      return 'col-span-4 sm:col-span-2 row-span-2';
    case 'vertical':
      return 'col-span-2 sm:col-span-1 row-span-2';
    default:
      return 'col-span-4 sm:col-span-2 row-span-1';
  }
}

function estimateClipPayloadWeight(clip: ClipboardItem): number {
  if (clip.fileSizeLabel) {
    const num = parseFloat(clip.fileSizeLabel);
    if (!Number.isNaN(num)) {
      if (clip.fileSizeLabel.toUpperCase().includes('MB')) return num * 1024 * 1024;
      if (clip.fileSizeLabel.toUpperCase().includes('KB')) return num * 1024;
    }
  }
  if (clip.previewImageUrl) return 512 * 1024 + clip.text.length;
  return clip.text.length;
}

function categoryGroupHeading(cat: ClipboardItem['category']): string {
  switch (cat) {
    case 'image':
    case 'video':
    case 'audio':
      return 'Images & Media';
    case 'code':
    case 'text':
      return 'Text & Code Snippets';
    case 'color':
    case 'link':
      return 'Colors & Web Links';
    case 'document':
    case 'archive':
    case 'binary':
      return 'Files, Docs & Archives';
    default:
      return 'Other Clips';
  }
}

function blobToBase64(blob: Blob): Promise<string> {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onloadend = () => {
      const res = typeof reader.result === 'string' ? reader.result : '';
      const idx = res.indexOf(',');
      resolve(idx >= 0 ? res.slice(idx + 1) : res);
    };
    reader.onerror = () => reject(new Error('Failed to encode audio'));
    reader.readAsDataURL(blob);
  });
}

    setDraggedWidgetId(null);
  }, [activeTab]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!stopwatchRunning) return;
    let last = performance.now();
    let raf = 0;
    const tick = (now: number) => {
      const dt = now - last;
      last = now;
      setStopwatchMs((prev) => prev + dt);
      raf = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    };
    raf = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    return () => cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
  }, [stopwatchRunning]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (activeTab !== 'mirror' || isLauncherOpen) {
      if (streamRef.current) {
        streamRef.current.getTracks().forEach((t) => t.stop());
        streamRef.current = null;
      }
      setCameraActive(false);
      setCameraStatus('idle');
    }
  }, [activeTab, isLauncherOpen]);

  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      if (streamRef.current) {
        streamRef.current.getTracks().forEach((t) => t.stop());
        streamRef.current = null;
      }
      if (noteRecorderRef.current && noteRecorderRef.current.state !== 'inactive') {
        noteRecorderRef.current.stop();
      }
      if (notedownSystemStreamRef.current) {
        notedownSystemStreamRef.current.getTracks().forEach((t) => t.stop());
        notedownSystemStreamRef.current = null;
      }
    };
  }, []);

  const runNotedownSummarize = async (opts: {
    transcriptOverride?: string;
    audioBase64?: string;
    mimeType?: string;
  }) => {
    if (!preferences.aiEnabled) {
      onTriggerQuickActionToast('Enable Search & AI in Settings to run Notedown');
      return;
    }
    setNotedownGenerating(true);
    playTactilePop('tab');
    try {
      const sourceText =
        opts.transcriptOverride ??
        (notedownRawTranscript.trim() || noteContent.trim());
      const resp = await fetch('/api/ai/notedown', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          rawTranscript: sourceText,
          audioBase64: opts.audioBase64,
          mimeType: opts.mimeType,
          formatStyle: preferences.notedownFormat ?? 'structured_md',
        }),
      });
      const data = (await resp.json()) as {
        markdown?: string;
        transcript?: string;
        error?: string;
      };
      if (!resp.ok || data.error) {
        throw new Error(data.error || 'Notedown summary failed');
      }
      if (data.transcript) {
        setNotedownRawTranscript(data.transcript);
      }
      if (data.markdown) {
        onChangeNoteContent(data.markdown);
        setNoteViewMode('preview');
        onTriggerQuickActionToast('Notedown structured Markdown ready');
        playTactilePop('confirm');
      }
    } catch (err) {
      onTriggerQuickActionToast(
        err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Notedown could not summarize audio'
      );
    } finally {
      setNotedownGenerating(false);
    }
  };

  const handleToggleNotedownSystemAudio = async () => {
    if (!preferences.aiEnabled) {
      onTriggerQuickActionToast('AI & Notedown are disabled in Settings');
      return;
    }
    if (notedownSystemActive && noteRecorderRef.current) {
      noteRecorderRef.current.stop();
      setNotedownSystemActive(false);
      return;
    }
    try {
      if (!navigator.mediaDevices?.getDisplayMedia) {
        onTriggerQuickActionToast('System audio capture requires a desktop browser');
        return;
      }
      const displayStream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getDisplayMedia({
        video: true,
        audio: true,
      });
      const audioTracks = displayStream.getAudioTracks();
      displayStream.getVideoTracks().forEach((vt) => vt.stop());

      if (audioTracks.length === 0) {
        displayStream.getTracks().forEach((t) => t.stop());
        onTriggerQuickActionToast('Enable "Share tab/system audio" in the picker for Notedown');
        return;
      }

      const audioStream = new MediaStream(audioTracks);
      notedownSystemStreamRef.current = audioStream;
      const preferredMime = MediaRecorder.isTypeSupported('audio/webm;codecs=opus')
        ? 'audio/webm;codecs=opus'
        : 'audio/webm';
      const recorder = new MediaRecorder(audioStream, { mimeType: preferredMime });
      noteChunksRef.current = [];
      recorder.ondataavailable = (ev) => {
        if (ev.data.size > 0) noteChunksRef.current.push(ev.data);
      };
      recorder.onstop = async () => {
        audioStream.getTracks().forEach((t) => t.stop());
        notedownSystemStreamRef.current = null;
        setNotedownSystemActive(false);
        const blob = new Blob(noteChunksRef.current, { type: preferredMime });
        if (blob.size > 0) {
          const b64 = await blobToBase64(blob);
          await runNotedownSummarize({ audioBase64: b64, mimeType: preferredMime });
        }
      };
      audioTracks[0].onended = () => {
        if (recorder.state !== 'inactive') recorder.stop();
      };
      noteRecorderRef.current = recorder;
      recorder.start(300);
      setNotedownSystemActive(true);
      playTactilePop('toggleOn');
    } catch {
      onTriggerQuickActionToast('System audio capture cancelled');
    }
  };

  const handleDownloadNoteMarkdown = () => {
    const content = noteContent.trim();
    if (!content) return;
    const blob = new Blob([content], { type: 'text/markdown;charset=utf-8' });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = url;
    a.download = `pill-notedown-${new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)}.md`;
    document.body.appendChild(a);
    a.click();
    document.body.removeChild(a);
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    playTactilePop('confirm');
  };

  const handleToggleNoteDictation = async () => {
    if (!preferences.aiEnabled) {
      onTriggerQuickActionToast('AI & Voice Dictation are disabled in Settings');
      return;
    }
    if (dictatingNote && noteRecorderRef.current) {
      noteRecorderRef.current.stop();
      setDictatingNote(false);
      return;
    }
    try {
      if (!navigator.mediaDevices?.getUserMedia) {
        onTriggerQuickActionToast('Microphone unavailable in this browser context');
        return;
      }
      const stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({ audio: true });
      const preferredMime = MediaRecorder.isTypeSupported('audio/webm;codecs=opus')
        ? 'audio/webm;codecs=opus'
        : 'audio/webm';
      const recorder = MediaRecorder.isTypeSupported(preferredMime)
        ? new MediaRecorder(stream, { mimeType: preferredMime })
        : new MediaRecorder(stream);
      noteChunksRef.current = [];
      recorder.ondataavailable = (ev) => {
        if (ev.data.size > 0) noteChunksRef.current.push(ev.data);
      };
      recorder.onstop = async () => {
        const actualMime = recorder.mimeType || preferredMime || 'audio/webm';
        stream.getTracks().forEach((t) => t.stop());
        const blob = new Blob(noteChunksRef.current, { type: actualMime });
        if (blob.size === 0) return;
        setTranscribingNote(true);
        try {
          const audioBase64 = await blobToBase64(blob);
          const resp = await fetch('/api/ai/transcribe', {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
            body: JSON.stringify({ audioBase64, mimeType: actualMime }),
          });
          const data = (await resp.json()) as { transcript?: string; error?: string };
          if (!resp.ok || data.error) {
            throw new Error(data.error || 'Dictation failed');
          }
          if (data.transcript) {
            setNotedownRawTranscript((prev) =>
              prev ? `${prev}\n${data.transcript}` : data.transcript!
            );
            onChangeNoteContent(
              noteContent.trim() ? `${noteContent.trim()}\n${data.transcript}` : data.transcript
            );
            playTactilePop('confirm');
          }
        } catch (err) {
          onTriggerQuickActionToast(
            err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Voice dictation failed'
          );
        } finally {
          setTranscribingNote(false);
        }
      };
      noteRecorderRef.current = recorder;
      recorder.start(250);
      setDictatingNote(true);
      playTactilePop('toggleOn');
    } catch {
      onTriggerQuickActionToast('Microphone permission denied or unavailable');
    }
  };

  const handleEnableCamera = async () => {
    if (cameraActive && streamRef.current) {
      streamRef.current.getTracks().forEach((t) => t.stop());
      streamRef.current = null;
      setCameraActive(false);
      setCameraStatus('idle');
      return;
    }
    try {
      if (!navigator.mediaDevices?.getUserMedia) {
        setCameraStatus('unavailable');
        return;
      }
      const stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({
        video: { width: { ideal: 640 }, height: { ideal: 360 } },
        audio: false,
      });
      streamRef.current = stream;
      setCameraActive(true);
      setCameraStatus('active');
      requestAnimationFrame(() => {
        if (videoRef.current) {
          videoRef.current.srcObject = stream;
        }
      });
    } catch {
      setCameraStatus('unavailable');
    }
  };

  const handleExportTrayZip = () => {
    if (trayFiles.length === 0) return;
    const zipBytes = createZipArchive(
      trayFiles.map((f) => ({
        path: f.name,
        content: f.contentPreview || `Pill Tray Reference: ${f.name} (${f.sizeLabel})`,
      }))
    );
    const blob = new Blob([zipBytes], { type: 'application/zip' });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = url;
    a.download = `Pill-Tray-${trayFiles.length}-files.zip`;
    document.body.appendChild(a);
    a.click();
    a.remove();
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    playTactilePop('confirm');
    setTrayFeedback(`Exported ${trayFiles.length} files as ZIP`);
    setTimeout(() => setTrayFeedback(null), 2400);
  };

  const handleMergeTrayReport = () => {
    if (trayFiles.length === 0) return;
    const mergedText = trayFiles
      .map((f, idx) => `--- [${idx + 1}] ${f.name} (${f.sizeLabel}) ---\n${f.contentPreview}`)
      .join('\n\n');
    const blob = new Blob([mergedText], { type: 'text/plain;charset=utf-8' });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = url;
    a.download = 'Tray-Merged-Bundle.txt';
    document.body.appendChild(a);
    a.click();
    a.remove();
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    playTactilePop('confirm');
    setTrayFeedback('Merged bundle downloaded');
    setTimeout(() => setTrayFeedback(null), 2400);
  };

  const handleFilePickerChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const list = e.target.files;
    if (!list || list.length === 0) return;
    const incoming: TrayFileItem[] = Array.from(list).map((file, idx) => {
      const ext = file.name.includes('.')
        ? file.name.split('.').pop()!.toUpperCase().slice(0, 5)
        : 'FILE';
      const kb = Math.max(0.4, file.size / 1024);
      return {
        id: `picked-${Date.now()}-${idx}`,
        name: file.name,
        extension: ext,
        sizeLabel: kb >= 1024 ? `${(kb / 1024).toFixed(1)} MB` : `${kb.toFixed(1)} KB`,
        addedAt: 'Just now',
        pinned: false,
        contentPreview: `Local file linked in Tray: ${file.name}`,
      };
    });
    onAddTrayFiles(incoming);
    playTactilePop('drop');
    e.target.value = '';
  };

  const handleUniversalClipboardFiles = (files: FileList | null) => {
    if (!files || files.length === 0) return;
    Array.from(files).forEach((file) => {
      const meta = classifyClipboardPayload(file.name, file.type);
      const kb = Math.max(0.2, file.size / 1024);
      const sizeStr = kb >= 1024 ? `${(kb / 1024).toFixed(1)} MB` : `${kb.toFixed(1)} KB`;
      const previewUrl = file.type.startsWith('image/') ? URL.createObjectURL(file) : undefined;
      onAddClipboardItem({
        text: file.name,
        source: 'Universal File Copy',
        category: meta.category,
        badgeCode: meta.badgeCode,
        fileSizeLabel: `${sizeStr} Â· ${file.type || 'Binary File'}`,
        previewImageUrl: previewUrl,
      });
    });
    playTactilePop('drop');
  };

  const handleCopyText = async (id: string, text: string) => {
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard?.writeText(text);
    } catch {
      // Ignore fallback
    }
    setCopiedId(id);
    playTactilePop('confirm');
    setTimeout(() => setCopiedId((curr) => (curr === id ? null : curr)), 1600);
  };

  const handleAddClipSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const trimmed = newClipDraft.trim();
    if (!trimmed) {
      setClipError('Enter text, hex color, URL, code, or filename');
      return;
    }
    setClipError('');
    const classified = classifyClipboardPayload(trimmed);
    onAddClipboardItem({
      text: trimmed,
      source: 'Universal Clip',
      category: classified.category,
      badgeCode: classified.badgeCode,
      accentHex: classified.accentHex,
    });
    setNewClipDraft('');
    playTactilePop('confirm');
  };

  // Compute visible tabs (Built-in + Enabled Extra Custom Widget Tabs)
  const visibleBuiltinTabs: PillTab[] = preferences.tabOrder.filter(
    (t) => preferences.enabledTabs[t] && (t !== 'ai' || preferences.aiEnabled)
  );
  const enabledCustomTabs = (preferences.customWidgetTabs ?? []).filter((ct) => ct.enabled);
  const visibleTabs: PillTab[] = [
    ...visibleBuiltinTabs,
    ...enabledCustomTabs.map((ct) => ct.id),
  ];

  const getTabDisplayLabel = (tabId: PillTab): string => {
    if (!isBuiltinTab(tabId)) {
      const found = (preferences.customWidgetTabs ?? []).find((ct) => ct.id === tabId);
      return found?.name || 'Widgets';
    }
    const customName = preferences.customTabNames?.[tabId]?.trim();
    const base = customName || BUILTIN_TAB_LABELS[tabId];
    if (tabId === 'tray' && trayFiles.length > 0) {
      return `${base} (${trayFiles.length})`;
    }
    return base;
  };

  // Filtered + Sorted + Grouped Clipboard items
  const sortedClips = useMemo(() => {
    const filtered = clipboardItems.filter((item) => {
      const matchesQuery =
        item.text.toLowerCase().includes(clipSearch.toLowerCase()) ||
        item.source.toLowerCase().includes(clipSearch.toLowerCase()) ||
        item.badgeCode.toLowerCase().includes(clipSearch.toLowerCase());
      if (!matchesQuery) return false;

      if (clipFilter === 'text_code') return item.category === 'text' || item.category === 'code';
      if (clipFilter === 'media') {
        return item.category === 'image' || item.category === 'video' || item.category === 'audio';
      }
      if (clipFilter === 'files') {
        return (
          item.category === 'document' ||
          item.category === 'archive' ||
          item.category === 'binary'
        );
      }
      if (clipFilter === 'colors_links') {
        return item.category === 'color' || item.category === 'link';
      }
      return true;
    });

    const sortMode: ClipboardSortBy = preferences.clipboardSortBy ?? 'newest';
    const indexed = filtered.map((item, idx) => ({ item, idx }));

    indexed.sort((a, b) => {
      if (sortMode === 'oldest') {
        return b.idx - a.idx;
      }
      if (sortMode === 'pinned') {
        if (a.item.pinned !== b.item.pinned) return a.item.pinned ? -1 : 1;
        return a.idx - b.idx;
      }
      if (sortMode === 'type') {
        const cmp = a.item.badgeCode.localeCompare(b.item.badgeCode);
        return cmp !== 0 ? cmp : a.idx - b.idx;
      }
      if (sortMode === 'size') {
        return estimateClipPayloadWeight(b.item) - estimateClipPayloadWeight(a.item);
      }
      // newest (default)
      return a.idx - b.idx;
    });

    return indexed.map((entry) => entry.item);
  }, [clipboardItems, clipSearch, clipFilter, preferences.clipboardSortBy]);

  const groupedClipSections = useMemo(() => {
    const groupMode: ClipboardGroupBy = preferences.clipboardGroupBy ?? 'none';
    if (groupMode === 'none') {
      return [{ heading: '', items: sortedClips }];
    }
    const map = new Map<string, ClipboardItem[]>();
    for (const item of sortedClips) {
      const h = categoryGroupHeading(item.category);
      const bucket = map.get(h);
      if (bucket) bucket.push(item);
      else map.set(h, [item]);
    }
    return Array.from(map.entries()).map(([heading, items]) => ({ heading, items }));
  }, [sortedClips, preferences.clipboardGroupBy]);

  const lyricIndex =
    currentTrack.lyrics.length > 0
      ? Math.floor(
          (trackProgressSec / Math.max(1, currentTrack.durationSec)) * currentTrack.lyrics.length
        ) % currentTrack.lyrics.length
      : 0;

  const cardSurfaceCls = isLight
    ? 'bg-stone-900/[0.05] border-stone-900/15 text-stone-900'
    : 'bg-white/[0.04] border-white/[0.08] text-slate-100';

  // Active Widget Page Resolver (supports both built-in 'home' AND any 'custom_*' extra widget tab!)
  const activeCustomTabObj = !isBuiltinTab(activeTab)
    ? (preferences.customWidgetTabs ?? []).find((ct) => ct.id === activeTab)
    : undefined;

  const isWidgetCanvasTab = activeTab === 'home' || Boolean(activeCustomTabObj);

  const pageWidgets: HomeWidgetId[] = activeCustomTabObj
    ? activeCustomTabObj.widgets
    : preferences.homeWidgets.length > 0
      ? preferences.homeWidgets
      : (['music', 'voice_search', 'timers', 'network_mini', 'quick_actions'] as HomeWidgetId[]);

  const getWidgetSize = (wid: HomeWidgetId): WidgetSizeVariant => {
    if (activeCustomTabObj) {
      return (
        activeCustomTabObj.widgetSizes?.[wid] ??
        preferences.widgetSizes?.[wid] ??
        HOME_WIDGET_CATALOG.find((c) => c.id === wid)?.defaultSize ??
        'medium'
      );
    }
    return (
      preferences.widgetSizes?.[wid] ??
      HOME_WIDGET_CATALOG.find((c) => c.id === wid)?.defaultSize ??
      'medium'
    );
  };

  const updatePageWidgetsAndSizes = (
    nextWidgets: HomeWidgetId[],
    nextSizePatch?: Partial<Record<HomeWidgetId, WidgetSizeVariant>>
  ) => {
    if (activeCustomTabObj) {
      const nextTabs = (preferences.customWidgetTabs ?? []).map((ct) =>
        ct.id === activeCustomTabObj.id
          ? {
              ...ct,
              widgets: nextWidgets,
              widgetSizes: nextSizePatch
                ? { ...(ct.widgetSizes ?? {}), ...nextSizePatch }
                : ct.widgetSizes,
            }
          : ct
      );
      onUpdatePreferences({ customWidgetTabs: nextTabs });
    } else {
      onUpdatePreferences({
        homeWidgets: nextWidgets,
        ...(nextSizePatch
          ? {
              widgetSizes: {
                ...preferences.widgetSizes,
                ...nextSizePatch,
              },
            }
          : {}),
      });
    }
  };

  const handleSetWidgetSize = (wid: HomeWidgetId, size: WidgetSizeVariant) => {
    playTactilePop('tab');
    updatePageWidgetsAndSizes(pageWidgets, { [wid]: size });
  };

  const handleRemoveWidgetFromPage = (wid: HomeWidgetId) => {
    if (pageWidgets.length <= 1) {
      onTriggerQuickActionToast('Keep at least one widget block on this page');
      return;
    }
    playTactilePop('collapse');
    updatePageWidgetsAndSizes(pageWidgets.filter((w) => w !== wid));
  };

  const handleAddWidgetToPage = (wid: HomeWidgetId) => {
    if (pageWidgets.includes(wid)) return;
    playTactilePop('confirm');
    const defSize = HOME_WIDGET_CATALOG.find((c) => c.id === wid)?.defaultSize ?? 'medium';
    updatePageWidgetsAndSizes([...pageWidgets, wid], { [wid]: getWidgetSize(wid) || defSize });
  };

  const handleHoverSwapWidget = (targetWid: HomeWidgetId) => {
    if (!draggedWidgetId || draggedWidgetId === targetWid) return;
    const fromIdx = pageWidgets.indexOf(draggedWidgetId);
    const toIdx = pageWidgets.indexOf(targetWid);
    if (fromIdx === -1 || toIdx === -1) return;
    const next = [...pageWidgets];
    const [moved] = next.splice(fromIdx, 1);
    next.splice(toIdx, 0, moved);
    updatePageWidgetsAndSizes(next);
    playTactilePop('tab');
  };

  const handleDropReorderWidget = (targetWid: HomeWidgetId) => {
    if (draggedWidgetId && draggedWidgetId !== targetWid) {
      handleHoverSwapWidget(targetWid);
    }
    setDraggedWidgetId(null);
  };

  // Multi-Size Music Widget Renderer (Small 1x1, Medium 2x1, Large 2x2, Vertical 1x2)
  const renderMusicWidgetBody = (size: WidgetSizeVariant) => {
    const lyricsOn = preferences.musicLyricsVisible !== false;

    // 1. SMALL (1Ã—1 Compact Square) â€” Zero wasted space!
    if (size === 'small') {
      return (
        <div className="flex flex-col justify-between h-full gap-1.5">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0">
            <ResilientThumb
              src={currentTrack.coverUrl}
              alt={currentTrack.title}
              className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg shrink-0 ring-1 ring-white/15"
            />
            <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
              <div className="text-[11px] font-semibold truncate leading-tight">
                {currentTrack.title}
              </div>
              <div className="text-[10px] opacity-65 truncate">{currentTrack.artist}</div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {lyricsOn && currentTrack.lyrics[lyricIndex] && (
            <div className="text-[10px] italic opacity-80 truncate px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-black/20">
              &ldquo;{currentTrack.lyrics[lyricIndex]}&rdquo;
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-1 pt-0.5">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                playTactilePop('tab');
                onPrevTrack();
              }}
              aria-label="Previous track"
              className="p-1 rounded-md hover:bg-white/10"
            >
              <SkipBack className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                playTactilePop(isPlaying ? 'toggleOff' : 'toggleOn');
                onTogglePlay();
              }}
              aria-label={isPlaying ? 'Pause music' : 'Play music'}
              style={{ backgroundColor: palette.primaryHex }}
              className={`p-1.5 rounded-full ${palette.onPrimaryTextClass}`}
            >
              {isPlaying ? <Pause className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> : <Play className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />}
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                playTactilePop('tab');
                onNextTrack();
              }}
              aria-label="Next track"
              className="p-1 rounded-md hover:bg-white/10"
            >
              <SkipForward className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      );
    }

    // 2. VERTICAL (1Ã—2 Tall Pillar) â€” Apple Control Center Vertical Music Column
    if (size === 'vertical') {
      return (
        <div className="flex flex-col justify-between h-full gap-2">
          <div className="flex flex-col items-center text-center gap-1.5 pt-0.5">
            <ResilientThumb
              src={currentTrack.coverUrl}
              alt={currentTrack.title}
              className="w-14 h-14 rounded-2xl shrink-0 ring-1 ring-white/20 shadow-md"
            />
            <div className="w-full min-w-0">
              <div className="text-xs font-semibold truncate">{currentTrack.title}</div>
              <div className="text-[10px] opacity-70 truncate">{currentTrack.artist}</div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {expandedFullLyrics ? (
            <div className="flex-1 min-h-0 overflow-y-auto pill-scroll rounded-xl bg-black/30 border border-white/10 p-2 space-y-1 text-[10px]">
              {currentTrack.lyrics.map((line, idx) => (
                <button
                  key={`${idx}-${line}`}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() =>
                    onSeekTrack(
                      Math.floor((idx / Math.max(1, currentTrack.lyrics.length)) * currentTrack.durationSec)
                    )
                  }
                  style={idx === lyricIndex ? { color: palette.primaryHex } : undefined}
                  className={`block w-full text-left leading-snug transition-opacity ${
                    idx === lyricIndex ? 'font-bold opacity-100' : 'opacity-55 hover:opacity-90'
                  }`}
                >
                  {line}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          ) : lyricsOn ? (
            <div className="px-2 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-black/25 border border-white/[0.07] text-[10px] italic text-center line-clamp-2">
              &ldquo;{currentTrack.lyrics[lyricIndex]}&rdquo;
            </div>
          ) : (
            <FluidMusicVisualizer
              style={preferences.musicVisualizer}
              isPlaying={isPlaying}
              accentHex={palette.primaryHex}
              secondaryHex={palette.secondaryHex}
              compact
              lowPowerEco={preferences.featureFlags.lowRamEcoMode}
            />
          )}

          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <LivingProgressBar
              currentSec={trackProgressSec}
              durationSec={currentTrack.durationSec}
              isPlaying={isPlaying}
              accentHex={palette.primaryHex}
              secondaryHex={palette.secondaryHex}
              sliderStyle={preferences.musicSliderStyle}
              livingGlowEnabled={preferences.featureFlags.livingProgressBar}
              glowIntensity={preferences.sliderGlowIntensity}
              lowPowerEco={preferences.featureFlags.lowRamEcoMode}
              onSeek={onSeekTrack}
            />
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-1">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() =>
                  onUpdatePreferences({ musicLyricsVisible: !lyricsOn })
                }
                title={lyricsOn ? 'Turn inline lyrics off' : 'Turn inline lyrics on'}
                className={`p-1 rounded-md text-[9px] font-semibold ${
                  lyricsOn ? 'bg-white/20 text-white' : 'opacity-60 hover:opacity-100'
                }`}
              >
                <MessageSquareQuote className="w-3 h-3" />
              </button>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={onPrevTrack}
                  aria-label="Previous track"
                  className="p-1 rounded-md hover:bg-white/10"
                >
                  <SkipBack className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={onTogglePlay}
                  aria-label={isPlaying ? 'Pause music' : 'Play music'}
                  style={{ backgroundColor: palette.primaryHex }}
                  className={`p-1.5 rounded-full ${palette.onPrimaryTextClass}`}
                >
                  {isPlaying ? <Pause className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> : <Play className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />}
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={onNextTrack}
                  aria-label="Next track"
                  className="p-1 rounded-md hover:bg-white/10"
                >
                  <SkipForward className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                </button>
              </div>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setExpandedFullLyrics((v) => !v)}
                title={expandedFullLyrics ? 'Collapse full lyrics' : 'Expand full lyrics'}
                className="p-1 rounded-md opacity-75 hover:opacity-100 hover:bg-white/10"
              >
                {expandedFullLyrics ? (
                  <Minimize2 className="w-3 h-3" />
                ) : (
                  <Maximize2 className="w-3 h-3" />
                )}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      );
    }

    // 3. MEDIUM (2Ã—1 Space-Efficient Wide Block â€” Default!)
    if (size === 'medium') {
      return (
        <div className="flex flex-col justify-between h-full gap-1.5">
          {/* Top Compact Header: Artwork + Track + Lyrics On/Off + Expand + Inline Transport */}
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0 flex-1">
              <ResilientThumb
                src={currentTrack.coverUrl}
                alt={`${currentTrack.title} album art`}
                className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl shrink-0 ring-1 ring-white/15"
              />
              <div className="min-w-0">
                <div className="text-xs font-semibold truncate leading-tight">
                  {currentTrack.title}
                </div>
                <div className="text-[11px] opacity-70 truncate">
                  {currentTrack.artist}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center gap-1 shrink-0">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  playTactilePop('tab');
                  onUpdatePreferences({ musicLyricsVisible: !lyricsOn });
                  if (lyricsOn) setExpandedFullLyrics(false);
                }}
                aria-pressed={lyricsOn}
                title={lyricsOn ? 'Hide inline lyrics' : 'Show inline lyrics'}
                style={
                  lyricsOn
                    ? {
                        backgroundColor: `${palette.primaryHex}26`,
                        borderColor: `${palette.primaryHex}66`,
                      }
                    : undefined
                }
                className={`px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-medium rounded-md border transition-colors ${
                  lyricsOn
                    ? 'text-white font-semibold'
                    : 'border-transparent opacity-65 hover:opacity-100 hover:bg-white/10'
                }`}
              >
                Lyrics
              </button>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setExpandedFullLyrics((v) => !v)}
                aria-label={expandedFullLyrics ? 'Collapse full lyrics' : 'Expand full lyrics'}
                title={expandedFullLyrics ? 'Collapse full lyrics' : 'Expand full lyrics'}
                className={`p-1 rounded-md transition-colors ${
                  expandedFullLyrics
                    ? 'bg-white/20 text-white'
                    : 'opacity-70 hover:opacity-100 hover:bg-white/10'
                }`}
              >
                {expandedFullLyrics ? (
                  <Minimize2 className="w-3 h-3" />
                ) : (
                  <Maximize2 className="w-3 h-3" />
                )}
              </button>

              <div className="flex items-center gap-0.5 pl-0.5">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    playTactilePop('tab');
                    onPrevTrack();
                  }}
                  aria-label="Previous track"
                  className="p-1 rounded-md hover:bg-white/10"
                >
                  <SkipBack className="w-3.5 h-3.5" strokeWidth={iconMetrics.strokeWidth} />
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    playTactilePop(isPlaying ? 'toggleOff' : 'toggleOn');
                    onTogglePlay();
                  }}
                  aria-label={isPlaying ? 'Pause music' : 'Play music'}
                  style={{ backgroundColor: palette.primaryHex }}
                  className={`p-1.5 rounded-full transition-transform active:scale-95 ${palette.onPrimaryTextClass}`}
                >
                  {isPlaying ? (
                    <Pause className="w-3.5 h-3.5" strokeWidth={iconMetrics.strokeWidth} />
                  ) : (
                    <Play className="w-3.5 h-3.5" strokeWidth={iconMetrics.strokeWidth} />
                  )}
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    playTactilePop('tab');
                    onNextTrack();
                  }}
                  aria-label="Next track"
                  className="p-1 rounded-md hover:bg-white/10"
                >
                  <SkipForward className="w-3.5 h-3.5" strokeWidth={iconMetrics.strokeWidth} />
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Fused Lyric + Living Scrubber Capsule (or Expandable Full Lyrics View) */}
          {expandedFullLyrics ? (
            <div className="max-h-24 overflow-y-auto pill-scroll px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-black/35 border border-white/10 space-y-1">
              {currentTrack.lyrics.map((line, idx) => (
                <button
                  key={`${idx}-${line}`}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() =>
                    onSeekTrack(
                      Math.floor(
                        (idx / Math.max(1, currentTrack.lyrics.length)) * currentTrack.durationSec
                      )
                    )
                  }
                  style={idx === lyricIndex ? { color: palette.primaryHex } : undefined}
                  className={`block w-full text-left text-[11px] leading-snug transition-opacity ${
                    idx === lyricIndex ? 'font-semibold opacity-100' : 'opacity-55 hover:opacity-90'
                  }`}
                >
                  &ldquo;{line}&rdquo;
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-black/25 border border-white/[0.07] flex flex-col gap-1">
              {lyricsOn && (
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                  <span className="text-[11px] opacity-90 italic truncate select-text">
                    &ldquo;{currentTrack.lyrics[lyricIndex]}&rdquo;
                  </span>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setExpandedFullLyrics(true)}
                    style={{ color: isLight ? '#9a3412' : palette.primaryHex }}
                    className="text-[10px] font-semibold shrink-0 hover:underline"
                  >
                    Full Lyrics
                  </button>
                </div>
              )}
              <LivingProgressBar
                currentSec={trackProgressSec}
                durationSec={currentTrack.durationSec}
                isPlaying={isPlaying}
                accentHex={palette.primaryHex}
                secondaryHex={palette.secondaryHex}
                sliderStyle={preferences.musicSliderStyle}
                livingGlowEnabled={preferences.featureFlags.livingProgressBar}
                glowIntensity={preferences.sliderGlowIntensity}
                lowPowerEco={preferences.featureFlags.lowRamEcoMode}
                onSeek={onSeekTrack}
              />
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      );
    }

    // 4. LARGE (2Ã—2 Full Studio Player â€” Zero Wasted Space, Full Synchronized Lyrics or Visualizer + Fused Scrubber)
    return (
      <div className="flex flex-col justify-between h-full gap-2">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 min-w-0">
            <ResilientThumb
              src={currentTrack.coverUrl}
              alt={`${currentTrack.title} album art`}
              className="w-11 h-11 rounded-xl shrink-0 ring-1 ring-white/15"
            />
            <div className="min-w-0">
              <div className="text-sm font-semibold truncate">{currentTrack.title}</div>
              <div className="text-xs opacity-70 truncate">
                {currentTrack.artist} Â· {currentTrack.album}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-1 shrink-0">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                playTactilePop('tab');
                onUpdatePreferences({ musicLyricsVisible: !lyricsOn });
                if (lyricsOn) setExpandedFullLyrics(false);
              }}
              aria-pressed={lyricsOn}
              style={
                lyricsOn
                  ? {
                      backgroundColor: `${palette.primaryHex}26`,
                      borderColor: `${palette.primaryHex}66`,
                    }
                  : undefined
              }
              className={`px-2 py-1 text-[10px] font-semibold rounded-lg border transition-colors ${
                lyricsOn
                  ? 'text-white'
                  : 'border-white/10 opacity-70 hover:opacity-100 hover:bg-white/10'
              }`}
            >
              {lyricsOn ? 'Lyrics On' : 'Lyrics Off'}
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setExpandedFullLyrics((v) => !v)}
              aria-label={expandedFullLyrics ? 'Collapse full lyrics' : 'Expand full lyrics'}
              title={expandedFullLyrics ? 'Collapse full lyrics' : 'Expand full synchronized lyrics'}
              className={`px-2 py-1 rounded-lg text-[10px] font-medium flex items-center gap-1 transition-colors ${
                expandedFullLyrics
                  ? 'bg-white/20 text-white font-semibold'
                  : 'bg-white/8 hover:bg-white/15 opacity-85'
              }`}
            >
              {expandedFullLyrics ? (
                <>
                  <Minimize2 className="w-3 h-3" />
                  <span>Compact</span>
                </>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <Maximize2 className="w-3 h-3" />
                  <span>Expand</span>
                </>
              )}
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setShowAudioMenu((v) => !v)}
              aria-label="Choose audio output device"
              className="p-1.5 rounded-lg opacity-75 hover:opacity-100 hover:bg-white/10"
            >
              <Speaker className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Middle Studio Area: Audio Output Picker OR Full Scrollable Lyrics OR Visualizer + Fused Inline Lyric */}
        {showAudioMenu ? (
          <div className="my-0.5 bg-slate-950/90 text-slate-100 border border-white/10 rounded-xl p-1.5 space-y-0.5">
            {AUDIO_DEVICES.map((dev) => (
              <button
                key={dev}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  onChangeAudioDevice(dev);
                  setShowAudioMenu(false);
                }}
                className={`w-full flex items-center justify-between px-2.5 py-1 text-xs rounded-lg ${
                  audioDevice === dev ? 'bg-white/15 font-medium' : 'hover:bg-white/5'
                }`}
              >
                <span className="truncate">{dev}</span>
                {audioDevice === dev && (
                  <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5" style={{ color: palette.primaryHex }} />
                )}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        ) : expandedFullLyrics ? (
          <div className="flex-1 min-h-[84px] max-h-32 overflow-y-auto pill-scroll px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-black/35 border border-white/10 space-y-1.5 select-text">
            {currentTrack.lyrics.map((line, idx) => (
              <button
                key={`${idx}-${line}`}
                type="button"
                onClick={() =>
                  onSeekTrack(
                    Math.floor(
                      (idx / Math.max(1, currentTrack.lyrics.length)) * currentTrack.durationSec
                    )
                  )
                }
                style={idx === lyricIndex ? { color: palette.primaryHex } : undefined}
                className={`block w-full text-left text-xs leading-relaxed transition-all ${
                  idx === lyricIndex
                    ? 'font-semibold opacity-100 scale-[1.01]'
                    : 'opacity-55 hover:opacity-90'
                }`}
              >
                &ldquo;{line}&rdquo;
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col justify-center gap-1.5">
            <FluidMusicVisualizer
              style={preferences.musicVisualizer}
              isPlaying={isPlaying}
              accentHex={palette.primaryHex}
              secondaryHex={palette.secondaryHex}
              lowPowerEco={preferences.featureFlags.lowRamEcoMode}
            />
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Bottom Fused Lyric + Living Scrubber + Transport Row */}
        <div className="space-y-1.5">
          <div className="px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-black/25 border border-white/[0.07] flex flex-col gap-1.5">
            {lyricsOn && !expandedFullLyrics && (
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                <span className="text-[11px] opacity-90 italic truncate select-text">
                  &ldquo;{currentTrack.lyrics[lyricIndex]}&rdquo;
                </span>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setExpandedFullLyrics(true)}
                  style={{ color: isLight ? '#9a3412' : palette.primaryHex }}
                  className="text-[10px] font-semibold shrink-0 hover:underline"
                >
                  Full Lyrics
                </button>
              </div>
            )}
            <LivingProgressBar
              currentSec={trackProgressSec}
              durationSec={currentTrack.durationSec}
              isPlaying={isPlaying}
              accentHex={palette.primaryHex}
              secondaryHex={palette.secondaryHex}
              sliderStyle={preferences.musicSliderStyle}
              livingGlowEnabled={preferences.featureFlags.livingProgressBar}
              glowIntensity={preferences.sliderGlowIntensity}
              lowPowerEco={preferences.featureFlags.lowRamEcoMode}
              onSeek={onSeekTrack}
            />
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setShowAudioMenu((v) => !v)}
              className="text-[11px] opacity-75 hover:opacity-100 truncate max-w-[165px] flex items-center gap-1"
            >
              <Speaker className="w-3 h-3 shrink-0" />
              <span className="truncate">{audioDevice}</span>
            </button>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  playTactilePop('tab');
                  onPrevTrack();
                }}
                aria-label="Previous track"
                className="p-1.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-white/10 transition-colors"
              >
                <SkipBack className="w-4 h-4" strokeWidth={iconMetrics.strokeWidth} />
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  playTactilePop(isPlaying ? 'toggleOff' : 'toggleOn');
                  onTogglePlay();
                }}
                aria-label={isPlaying ? 'Pause music' : 'Play music'}
                style={{ backgroundColor: palette.primaryHex }}
                className={`p-2 rounded-full transition-transform active:scale-95 ${palette.onPrimaryTextClass}`}
              >
                {isPlaying ? (
                  <Pause className="w-4 h-4" strokeWidth={iconMetrics.strokeWidth} />
                ) : (
                  <Play className="w-4 h-4" strokeWidth={iconMetrics.strokeWidth} />
                )}
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  playTactilePop('tab');
                  onNextTrack();
                }}
                aria-label="Next track"
                className="p-1.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-white/10 transition-colors"
              >
                <SkipForward className="w-4 h-4" strokeWidth={iconMetrics.strokeWidth} />
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  };

  // Multi-Size Building-Block Widget Renderer for All Catalog Widgets
  const renderWidgetBlockContent = (wid: HomeWidgetId, size: WidgetSizeVariant) => {
    if (wid === 'music') {
      if (!preferences.featureFlags.nowPlaying) {
        return (
