// Shared module queue so a spoken wake phrase ("Astra turn on Night Light") immediately runs inside the morphed Pill
let pendingWakePhraseCommand: string | null = null;

const QUICK_ASTRA_PROMPTS = [
  'Turn off Wi-Fi & enable Night Light',
  'Set volume to 80% & start 5m timer',
  'Enable OS Privacy Mask for screen share',
  'Switch style kit to Cyberpunk',
];

/**
 * Reusable wrapper around the 60fps 3D Refractive Apple Siri Silk-Ribbon Sphere (`AstraSiriOrbCanvas`)
 * for Settings previews and header badges.
 */
export const AstraLiquidOrbVisual: React.FC<{
  active: boolean;
  processing: boolean;
  speaking: boolean;
  audioLevel?: number;
  size?: number;
  lowPowerEco?: boolean;
  style?: AstraOrbStyle;
  primaryHex?: string;
  secondaryHex?: string;
}> = ({
  active,
  processing,
  speaking,
  audioLevel = 0,
  size = 48,
  lowPowerEco = false,
  style = 'siri_rainbow',
}) => {
  const orbState: SiriOrbVisualState = processing
    ? 'thinking'
    : speaking
      ? 'speaking'
      : active
        ? 'listening'
        : 'idle';

  return (
    <AstraSiriOrbCanvas
      state={orbState}
      audioLevel={audioLevel}
      size={size}
      lowPowerEco={lowPowerEco}
      style={style}
    />
  );
};

/**
 * Headless zero-UI Wake-Word Daemon listening for "Astra" / "Astram" / "à°…à°¸à±à°¤à±à°°à°‚"
 * when Astra is not currently morphed open in the Pill.
 */
export const AstraWakeWordDaemon: React.FC<{
  isAstraOpen: boolean;
  aiEnabled: boolean;
  wakeWordEnabled: boolean;
  onSummonAstra: () => void;
}> = ({ isAstraOpen, aiEnabled, wakeWordEnabled, onSummonAstra }) => {
  const isOpenRef = useRef(isAstraOpen);
  isOpenRef.current = isAstraOpen;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isAstraOpen || !aiEnabled || !wakeWordEnabled) {
      return;
    }

    const SpeechCtor = getSpeechRecognitionCtor();
    if (!SpeechCtor) return;

    let cancelled = false;
    let rec: BrowserSpeechRecognitionInstance | null = null;

    const launchWakeDetector = () => {
      if (cancelled || isOpenRef.current) return;
      try {
        rec = new SpeechCtor();
        rec.continuous = true;
        rec.interimResults = true;
        rec.lang = 'en-US';

        rec.onresult = (ev: BrowserSpeechRecognitionEvent) => {
          let latest = '';
          for (let i = ev.resultIndex; i < ev.results.length; i++) {
            latest += ev.results[i][0].transcript + ' ';
          }
          const lower = latest.toLowerCase().trim();
          const wakeMatch = lower.match(/\b(?:hey\s+)?(?:astra|astram|à°…à°¸à±à°¤à±à°°à°‚)\b(.*)$/i);
          if (wakeMatch) {
            const trailingCmd = (wakeMatch[1] || '').trim();
            if (trailingCmd.length > 3) {
              pendingWakePhraseCommand = trailingCmd;
            }
            try {
              rec?.abort();
            } catch {
              // Ignore
            }
            playTactilePop('expand');
            onSummonAstra();
          }
        };

        rec.onerror = () => {
          // Quietly wait if microphone permission is not yet granted
        };

        rec.onend = () => {
          if (!cancelled && !isOpenRef.current && wakeWordEnabled) {
            window.setTimeout(() => {
              if (!cancelled && !isOpenRef.current) launchWakeDetector();
            }, 950);
          }
        };

        rec.start();
      } catch {
        // Browser may require gesture before mic auto-start
      }
    };

    launchWakeDetector();

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
      if (rec) {
        try {
          rec.onend = null;
          rec.abort();
        } catch {
          // Ignore
        }
      }
    };
  }, [isAstraOpen, aiEnabled, wakeWordEnabled, onSummonAstra]);

  return null;
};

export interface InPillAstraPanelProps {
  onClose: () => void;
  preferences: PillPreferences;
  onUpdatePreferences: (patch: Partial<PillPreferences>) => void;
  systemState: WindowsSystemState;
  volumeLevel: number;
  brightnessLevel: number;
  isPlaying: boolean;
  nightLight: boolean;
  keepAwake: boolean;
  micMuted: boolean;
  onExecuteActions: (actions: VoiceAgentAction[]) => void;
  onResultStateChange?: (hasResult: boolean) => void;
}

/**
 * In-Pill Astra (`à°…à°¸à±à°¤à±à°°à°‚` / `Astram`) Morph Surface.
 * Renders directly inside the existing Pill â€” never spawning a second separate window!
 */
export const InPillAstraPanel: React.FC<InPillAstraPanelProps> = ({
  onClose,
  preferences,
  onUpdatePreferences,
  systemState,
  volumeLevel,
  brightnessLevel,
  isPlaying,
  nightLight,
  keepAwake,
  micMuted,
  onExecuteActions,
  onResultStateChange,
}) => {
  const palette = resolveThemePalette(
    preferences.themeId,
    preferences.customPrimaryHex,
    preferences.customSecondaryHex
  );

  const [typedInput, setTypedInput] = useState('');
  const [liveInterimText, setLiveInterimText] = useState('');
  const [lastPrompt, setLastPrompt] = useState('');
  const [isListeningLive, setIsListeningLive] = useState(false);
  const [isProcessing, setIsProcessing] = useState(false);
  const [isSpeakingReply, setIsSpeakingReply] = useState(false);
  const [micAudioLevel, setMicAudioLevel] = useState(0);
  const [replyMarkdown, setReplyMarkdown] = useState<string>('');
  const [executedActions, setExecutedActions] = useState<VoiceAgentAction[]>([]);
  const [sources, setSources] = useState<AgentSource[]>([]);
  const [errorMsg, setErrorMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [copiedReply, setCopiedReply] = useState(false);
  const [showQuickChips, setShowQuickChips] = useState(false);

  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement | null>(null);
  const activeRecRef = useRef<BrowserSpeechRecognitionInstance | null>(null);
  const silenceTimerRef = useRef<number | null>(null);
  const fallbackRecorderRef = useRef<MediaRecorder | null>(null);
  const fallbackChunksRef = useRef<Blob[]>([]);
  const replyAudioRef = useRef<HTMLAudioElement | null>(null);
  const ttsAbortRef = useRef<AbortController | null>(null);
  const mountedRef = useRef(true);
  const meterStreamRef = useRef<MediaStream | null>(null);
  const meterCtxRef = useRef<AudioContext | null>(null);
  const meterRafRef = useRef<number>(0);

  const isProcessingRef = useRef(isProcessing);
  isProcessingRef.current = isProcessing;

  const hasResult = Boolean(
    (replyMarkdown || executedActions.length > 0 || errorMsg) && !isProcessing
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    onResultStateChange?.(hasResult || showQuickChips);
  }, [hasResult, showQuickChips, onResultStateChange]);

  const stopAudioMeter = useCallback(() => {
    if (meterRafRef.current) {
      cancelAnimationFrame(meterRafRef.current);
      meterRafRef.current = 0;
    }
    if (meterStreamRef.current) {
      meterStreamRef.current.getTracks().forEach((t) => t.stop());
      meterStreamRef.current = null;
    }
    if (meterCtxRef.current) {
      meterCtxRef.current.close().catch(() => {});
      meterCtxRef.current = null;
    }
    setMicAudioLevel(0);
  }, []);

  const startAudioMeter = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      if (!navigator.mediaDevices?.getUserMedia) return;
      const stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({ audio: true });
      if (!mountedRef.current) {
        stream.getTracks().forEach((t) => t.stop());
        return;
      }
      meterStreamRef.current = stream;
      const AudioCtx =
        window.AudioContext ||
        (window as unknown as { webkitAudioContext: typeof AudioContext }).webkitAudioContext;
      const ctx = new AudioCtx();
      meterCtxRef.current = ctx;
      const source = ctx.createMediaStreamSource(stream);
      const analyser = ctx.createAnalyser();
      analyser.fftSize = 128;
      source.connect(analyser);
      const data = new Uint8Array(analyser.frequencyBinCount);

      const sample = () => {
        if (!mountedRef.current || !meterCtxRef.current) return;
        analyser.getByteFrequencyData(data);
        let sum = 0;
        for (let i = 0; i < data.length; i++) sum += data[i];
        const avg = sum / (data.length * 255);
        setMicAudioLevel(Math.min(1, avg * 3.4));
        meterRafRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(sample);
      };
      meterRafRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(sample);
    } catch {
      // Optional visual amplitude meter; speech still works cleanly without it
    }
  }, []);

  const speakAssistantReply = useCallback(
    async (textToSpeak: string) => {
      if (!preferences.voiceAgentSpeakReplies || !mountedRef.current) return;
      try {
        ttsAbortRef.current?.abort();
        const abortCtrl = new AbortController();
        ttsAbortRef.current = abortCtrl;
        if (replyAudioRef.current) {
          replyAudioRef.current.pause();
          replyAudioRef.current = null;
        }
        setIsSpeakingReply(true);
        const resp = await fetch('/api/ai/tts', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            text: textToSpeak,
            voiceName: preferences.defaultLiveVoice,
          }),
          signal: abortCtrl.signal,
        });
        const data = (await resp.json()) as { audioWavBase64?: string };
        if (!mountedRef.current || abortCtrl.signal.aborted) {
          setIsSpeakingReply(false);
          return;
        }
        if (resp.ok && data.audioWavBase64) {
          const audio = new Audio(`data:audio/wav;base64,${data.audioWavBase64}`);
          replyAudioRef.current = audio;
          audio.onended = () => setIsSpeakingReply(false);
          audio.onerror = () => setIsSpeakingReply(false);
          await audio.play().catch(() => setIsSpeakingReply(false));
        } else {
          setIsSpeakingReply(false);
        }
      } catch {
        setIsSpeakingReply(false);
      }
    },
    [preferences.defaultLiveVoice, preferences.voiceAgentSpeakReplies]
  );

  const runAgentCommand = useCallback(
    async (commandText: string) => {
      const trimmed = commandText.trim();
      if (!trimmed || isProcessingRef.current || !preferences.aiEnabled) return;

      setErrorMsg(null);
      setLiveInterimText('');
      setLastPrompt(trimmed);
      setIsProcessing(true);
      playTactilePop('tab');

      try {
        const response = await fetch('/api/ai/agent', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            command: trimmed,
            stateSnapshot: {
              wifiEnabled: systemState.wifiEnabled,
              bluetoothEnabled: systemState.bluetoothEnabled,
              airplaneMode: systemState.airplaneMode,
              nightLight,
              batterySaver: systemState.batterySaver,
              dndEnabled: systemState.dndEnabled,
              keepAwake,
              micMuted,
              privacyMaskEnabled: preferences.privacyMaskEnabled,
              volumeLevel,
              brightnessLevel,
              isPlaying,
              themeId: preferences.themeId,
            },
          }),
        });

        const data = (await response.json()) as {
          reply?: string;
          actions?: VoiceAgentAction[];
          sources?: AgentSource[];
          error?: string;
        };

        if (!response.ok || data.error) {
          throw new Error(data.error || `Astra error (${response.status})`);
        }

        if (!mountedRef.current) return;

        const nextActions = data.actions ?? [];
        if (nextActions.length > 0) {
          onExecuteActions(nextActions);
        }
        setExecutedActions(nextActions);
        setSources(data.sources ?? []);
        const reply = data.reply || 'Done.';
        setReplyMarkdown(reply);
        setTypedInput('');
        playTactilePop('confirm');
        speakAssistantReply(reply);
      } catch (err) {
        if (mountedRef.current) {
          setErrorMsg(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Unable to run Astra command.');
        }
      } finally {
        if (mountedRef.current) {
          setIsProcessing(false);
        }
      }
    },
    [
      brightnessLevel,
      isPlaying,
      keepAwake,
      micMuted,
      nightLight,
      onExecuteActions,
      preferences.aiEnabled,
      preferences.privacyMaskEnabled,
      preferences.themeId,
      speakAssistantReply,
      systemState.airplaneMode,
      systemState.batterySaver,
      systemState.bluetoothEnabled,
      systemState.dndEnabled,
      systemState.wifiEnabled,
      volumeLevel,
    ]
  );

  const stopActiveListening = useCallback(() => {
    if (silenceTimerRef.current !== null) {
      window.clearTimeout(silenceTimerRef.current);
      silenceTimerRef.current = null;
    }
    if (activeRecRef.current) {
      try {
        activeRecRef.current.onend = null;
        activeRecRef.current.stop();
      } catch {
        // Ignore
      }
      activeRecRef.current = null;
    }
    if (fallbackRecorderRef.current && fallbackRecorderRef.current.state !== 'inactive') {
      fallbackRecorderRef.current.stop();
    }
    stopAudioMeter();
    setIsListeningLive(false);
  }, [stopAudioMeter]);

  const startStreamingListen = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!preferences.aiEnabled || isListeningLive) return;
    setErrorMsg(null);

    const SpeechCtor = getSpeechRecognitionCtor();
    if (SpeechCtor) {
      try {
        const rec = new SpeechCtor();
        rec.continuous = true;
        rec.interimResults = true;
        rec.lang = 'en-US';

        let rollingTranscript = '';

        rec.onresult = (event: BrowserSpeechRecognitionEvent) => {
          let combined = '';
          for (let i = 0; i < event.results.length; i++) {
            combined += event.results[i][0].transcript;
          }
          rollingTranscript = combined.trim();
          setLiveInterimText(rollingTranscript);

          if (silenceTimerRef.current !== null) {
            window.clearTimeout(silenceTimerRef.current);
          }
          if (rollingTranscript.length > 1) {
            silenceTimerRef.current = window.setTimeout(() => {
              const toRun = rollingTranscript.trim();
              stopActiveListening();
              if (toRun) {
                runAgentCommand(toRun);
              }
            }, 1150);
          }
        };

        rec.onerror = () => {
          stopAudioMeter();
          setIsListeningLive(false);
        };

        rec.onend = () => {
          stopAudioMeter();
          setIsListeningLive(false);
          const finalSpoken = rollingTranscript.trim();
          if (finalSpoken && !isProcessingRef.current) {
            if (silenceTimerRef.current !== null) {
              window.clearTimeout(silenceTimerRef.current);
              silenceTimerRef.current = null;
            }
            runAgentCommand(finalSpoken);
          }
        };

        activeRecRef.current = rec;
        rec.start();
        setIsListeningLive(true);
        startAudioMeter();
        playTactilePop('toggleOn');
        return;
      } catch {
        // Fall through to MediaRecorder fallback
      }
    }

    try {
      if (!navigator.mediaDevices?.getUserMedia) return;
      const stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({ audio: true });
      const preferredMime = MediaRecorder.isTypeSupported('audio/webm;codecs=opus')
        ? 'audio/webm;codecs=opus'
        : 'audio/webm';
      const recorder = MediaRecorder.isTypeSupported(preferredMime)
        ? new MediaRecorder(stream, { mimeType: preferredMime })
        : new MediaRecorder(stream);

      fallbackChunksRef.current = [];
      recorder.ondataavailable = (ev) => {
        if (ev.data.size > 0) fallbackChunksRef.current.push(ev.data);
      };
      recorder.onstop = async () => {
        const actualMime = recorder.mimeType || preferredMime || 'audio/webm';
        stream.getTracks().forEach((t) => t.stop());
        stopAudioMeter();
        setIsListeningLive(false);
        const blob = new Blob(fallbackChunksRef.current, { type: actualMime });
        if (blob.size === 0 || !mountedRef.current) return;
        setIsProcessing(true);
        try {
          const audioBase64 = await blobToBase64(blob);
          const trResp = await fetch('/api/ai/transcribe', {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
            body: JSON.stringify({ audioBase64, mimeType: actualMime }),
          });
          const trData = (await trResp.json()) as { transcript?: string };
          const spoken = (trData.transcript ?? '').trim();
          setIsProcessing(false);
          if (spoken && mountedRef.current) {
            await runAgentCommand(spoken);
          }
        } catch {
          if (mountedRef.current) setIsProcessing(false);
        }
      };
      fallbackRecorderRef.current = recorder;
      recorder.start(250);
      setIsListeningLive(true);
      playTactilePop('toggleOn');
    } catch {
      // User can still type seamlessly
    }
  }, [
    isListeningLive,
    preferences.aiEnabled,
    runAgentCommand,
    startAudioMeter,
    stopActiveListening,
    stopAudioMeter,
  ]);

  useEffect(() => {
    mountedRef.current = true;
    if (preferences.aiEnabled) {
      if (pendingWakePhraseCommand) {
        const cmd = pendingWakePhraseCommand;
        pendingWakePhraseCommand = null;
        runAgentCommand(cmd);
      } else {
        startStreamingListen();
      }
      if (preferences.astraMode === 'orb_with_chat') {
        window.setTimeout(() => inputRef.current?.focus(), 70);
      }
    }

    return () => {
      mountedRef.current = false;
      onResultStateChange?.(false);
      stopActiveListening();
      ttsAbortRef.current?.abort();
      if (replyAudioRef.current) {
        replyAudioRef.current.pause();
        replyAudioRef.current = null;
      }
    };
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, []);

  const setAstraMode = (nextMode: AstraDisplayMode) => {
    playTactilePop('tab');
    onUpdatePreferences({ astraMode: nextMode });
    if (nextMode === 'orb_with_chat') {
      window.setTimeout(() => inputRef.current?.focus(), 70);
    }
  };

  const pressTimerRef = useRef<number | null>(null);
  const isLongPressRef = useRef(false);

  const handleOrbPointerDown = () => {
    isLongPressRef.current = false;
    pressTimerRef.current = window.setTimeout(() => {
      isLongPressRef.current = true;
      playTactilePop('expand');
      setAstraMode('orb_with_chat');
    }, 450);
  };

  const handleOrbPointerUp = () => {
    if (pressTimerRef.current !== null) {
      window.clearTimeout(pressTimerRef.current);
      pressTimerRef.current = null;
    }
    if (!isLongPressRef.current) {
      // Single click: toggle voice listening
      if (isListeningLive) stopActiveListening();
      else startStreamingListen();
    }
  };

  const handleOrbPointerCancel = () => {
    if (pressTimerRef.current !== null) {
      window.clearTimeout(pressTimerRef.current);
      pressTimerRef.current = null;
    }
  };

  const orbVisualState: SiriOrbVisualState = isProcessing
    ? 'thinking'
    : isSpeakingReply
      ? 'speaking'
      : isListeningLive
        ? 'listening'
        : 'idle';

  const activeTranscriptBubble = liveInterimText || lastPrompt;

  /* ==========================================================================
   * IN-PILL MODE 1: `orb_only` â€” Sleek, Clean Big Astra Orb (Only Orb, Zero Overhead)
   * Single tap: Speak / Listen Â· Long press (>450ms): Open Chat Studio
   * ======================================================================== */
  if (preferences.astraMode === 'orb_only') {
    return (
      <div
        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
        className="w-full h-full flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-0 text-slate-100 select-none relative bg-transparent"
      >
        <button
          type="button"
          onPointerDown={handleOrbPointerDown}
          onPointerUp={handleOrbPointerUp}
          onPointerCancel={handleOrbPointerCancel}
          onContextMenu={(e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            setAstraMode('orb_with_chat');
          }}
          title={
            isListeningLive
              ? 'Astra listening (Tap to stop Â· Long press for Chat)'
              : 'Tap to speak to Astra Â· Long press for Chat'
          }
          aria-label="Astra Voice Orb (Tap to speak, Long press for Chat)"
          className="rounded-full transition-transform active:scale-95 hover:scale-105 focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-sky-400 flex items-center justify-center cursor-pointer bg-transparent border-0 ring-0 shadow-none p-0 m-0"
        >
          <AstraSiriOrbCanvas
            state={orbVisualState}
            audioLevel={micAudioLevel}
            size={70}
            style={preferences.astraOrbStyle}
            lowPowerEco={preferences.featureFlags.lowRamEcoMode}
          />
        </button>

        {/* Inline Expanded Result if Astra answers while in compact mode */}
        {hasResult && (
          <div className="pt-1 mt-1 border-t border-white/10 max-h-[80px] overflow-y-auto pill-scroll space-y-1 w-full px-2 text-left">
            <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-200">
              <RichMarkdown content={replyMarkdown} />
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    );
  }

  /* ==========================================================================
   * IN-PILL MODE 2: `orb_with_captions` â€” Compact Orb + Live Streaming Subtitles
   * Frosted acrylic background matching the exact design language of the Pill
   * ======================================================================== */
  if (preferences.astraMode === 'orb_with_captions') {
    return (
      <div
        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
        className="w-full h-full flex items-center justify-between px-3 py-1.5 gap-2.5 text-slate-100 select-text"
      >
        {/* Compact Orb on the left */}
        <button
          type="button"
          onPointerDown={handleOrbPointerDown}
          onPointerUp={handleOrbPointerUp}
          onPointerCancel={handleOrbPointerCancel}
          title={
            isListeningLive
              ? 'Astra listening (Tap to stop Â· Long press for Chat)'
              : 'Tap to speak Â· Long press for Chat'
          }
          aria-label="Astra Voice Orb (Tap to speak, Long press for Chat)"
          className="shrink-0 rounded-full transition-transform active:scale-95 hover:scale-105 focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-sky-400 cursor-pointer"
        >
          <AstraSiriOrbCanvas
            state={orbVisualState}
            audioLevel={micAudioLevel}
            size={40}
            style={preferences.astraOrbStyle}
            lowPowerEco={preferences.featureFlags.lowRamEcoMode}
          />
        </button>

        {/* Dynamic Glassmorphism Live Captions Stream matching Pill Design Language */}
        <div className="flex-1 min-w-0 flex items-center">
          <div className="w-full px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-[#05070c]/85 border border-white/15 backdrop-blur-xl flex items-center gap-2 overflow-hidden shadow-2xl">
            {isProcessing ? (
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-medium text-sky-200 truncate">
                <Loader2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 animate-spin text-sky-400 shrink-0" />
                <span className="truncate">Thinkingâ€¦</span>
              </div>
            ) : liveInterimText ? (
              <div className="text-xs font-medium text-white truncate flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <span style={{ color: palette.primaryHex }} className="text-[10px] uppercase font-mono font-bold shrink-0">You:</span>
                <span className="truncate">&ldquo;{liveInterimText}&rdquo;</span>
              </div>
            ) : replyMarkdown ? (
              <div className="text-xs text-slate-200 truncate flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <span className="text-[10px] uppercase font-mono font-bold text-purple-400 shrink-0">Astra:</span>
                <span className="truncate">{String(replyMarkdown || '').replace(/[#*_`]/g, '')}</span>
              </div>
            ) : lastPrompt ? (
              <div className="text-xs text-slate-300 truncate flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <span style={{ color: palette.primaryHex }} className="text-[10px] uppercase font-mono font-bold shrink-0">You:</span>
                <span className="truncate">{lastPrompt}</span>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="text-xs text-slate-400 truncate flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <span style={{ color: palette.primaryHex }} className="font-semibold text-[11px]">Astra</span>
                <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400">Â· Speak or tap orbâ€¦</span>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Mini quick controls */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1 shrink-0">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setAstraMode('orb_with_chat')}
            title="Expand Full Chat (or long press orb)"
            aria-label="Expand Full Chat"
            className="w-7 h-7 rounded-full bg-white/8 hover:bg-white/16 flex items-center justify-center text-slate-300 hover:text-white transition-colors"
          >
            <MessageSquare className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            title="Return to Pill"
            aria-label="Close Astra"
            className="w-7 h-7 rounded-full bg-white/8 hover:bg-white/16 flex items-center justify-center text-slate-300 hover:text-white transition-colors"
          >
            <X className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  /* ==========================================================================
   * IN-PILL MODE 2: `orb_with_chat` â€” Morphs the existing Pill into Siri Orb + Chat Studio
   * ======================================================================== */
  return (
    <div
      onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
      className="relative w-full h-full flex flex-col justify-between px-4 py-3 text-slate-100 select-text"
    >
      {/* Top In-Pill Header */}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 shrink-0">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 min-w-0">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
              if (isListeningLive) stopActiveListening();
              else startStreamingListen();
            }}
            aria-label="Toggle Astra live voice"
            className="rounded-full shrink-0 transition-transform active:scale-95"
          >
            <AstraSiriOrbCanvas
              state={orbVisualState}
              audioLevel={micAudioLevel}
              size={38}
              style={preferences.astraOrbStyle}
              lowPowerEco={preferences.featureFlags.lowRamEcoMode}
            />
          </button>
          <div className="min-w-0">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-white">Astra</span>
              <span className="text-[10.5px] text-cyan-300 font-medium">à°…à°¸à±à°¤à±à°°à°‚</span>
              {preferences.privacyMaskEnabled && (
                <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[10px] text-emerald-300/90 ml-1">
                  <ShieldCheck className="w-3 h-3" />
                  <span>Mask On</span>
                </span>
              )}
            </div>
            <div className="text-[10.5px] text-slate-400 truncate">
              {isListeningLive
                ? 'Listening live inside Pill â€” pause speaking to execute'
                : 'In-Pill Siri Agent Â· Windows Controls & Grounded Web'}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-1 shrink-0">
          <button
            type="button"
            aria-pressed={preferences.voiceAgentSpeakReplies}
            onClick={() =>
              onUpdatePreferences({
                voiceAgentSpeakReplies: !preferences.voiceAgentSpeakReplies,
              })
            }
            title={preferences.voiceAgentSpeakReplies ? 'Spoken replies On' : 'Spoken replies Muted'}
            className="w-7 h-7 rounded-full bg-white/8 hover:bg-white/16 flex items-center justify-center text-slate-300 hover:text-white transition-colors"
          >
            {preferences.voiceAgentSpeakReplies ? (
              <Volume2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" style={{ color: palette.primaryHex }} />
            ) : (
              <VolumeX className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            )}
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setAstraMode('orb_only')}
            title="Switch to Compact In-Pill Siri Orb"
            aria-label="Switch to Compact In-Pill Siri Orb"
            className="w-7 h-7 rounded-full bg-white/8 hover:bg-white/16 flex items-center justify-center text-slate-300 hover:text-white transition-colors"
          >
            <Minimize2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            title="Close Astra and return Pill"
            aria-label="Close Astra"
            className="w-7 h-7 rounded-full bg-white/10 hover:bg-white/18 flex items-center justify-center text-slate-300 hover:text-white transition-colors"
          >
            <X className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Center Scrollable Conversation & Action Area */}
      <div className="flex-1 min-h-0 overflow-y-auto pill-scroll my-2 pr-1 space-y-2">
        {activeTranscriptBubble ? (
          <div className="flex justify-end">
            <div className="max-w-[85%] rounded-2xl bg-white/[0.1] border border-white/12 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs text-white leading-relaxed">
              {activeTranscriptBubble}
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="py-2 text-center">
            <div className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-200">
              Speak naturally or type below â€” the Pill itself is now Astra
            </div>
            <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
              Controls Wi-Fi, Bluetooth, Privacy Mask, Volume, Brightness, Timers &amp; Live Web Search
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {isProcessing && (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-sky-200 py-1">
            <Loader2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 animate-spin text-sky-300" />
            <span>Running commandâ€¦</span>
          </div>
        )}

        {executedActions.length > 0 && !isProcessing && (

                type="button"

                ) : (

                )}
                <span>{copiedReply ? 'Copied' : 'Copy'}</span>
              </button>

              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                {sources.slice(0, 2).map((src, idx) => (
                  <a
                    key={`${src.uri}-${idx}`}
                    href={src.uri}
                    target="_blank"
                    rel="noreferrer"
                    className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[10.5px] text-sky-300 hover:underline max-w-[130px] truncate"
                  >
                    <ExternalLink className="w-2.5 h-2.5 shrink-0" />
                    <span className="truncate">{src.domain || src.title}</span>
                  </a>
                ))}
                {lastPrompt && (
                  <a
                    href={`https://www.google.com/search?q=${encodeURIComponent(lastPrompt)}`}
                    target="_blank"
                    rel="noreferrer"
                    className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] text-sky-300 hover:underline"
                  >
                    <Search className="w-3 h-3" />
                    <span>Google</span>
                  </a>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {showQuickChips && (
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5 pt-1">
            {QUICK_ASTRA_PROMPTS.map((chip) => (
              <button
                key={chip}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  setShowQuickChips(false);
                  runAgentCommand(chip);
                }}
                className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-xl bg-white/[0.06] hover:bg-white/[0.14] border border-white/10 text-[11px] text-slate-200 text-left transition-colors"
              >
                {chip}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Bottom In-Pill Input Bar with Siri Iridescent Horizon Glow */}
      <div className="relative shrink-0 pt-1">
        <div
          style={{
            background:
              'linear-gradient(90deg, #38bdf8 0%, #a855f7 35%, #ec4899 68%, #fbbf24 100%)',
          }}
          className={`pointer-events-none absolute inset-x-8 -bottom-1 h-2 rounded-full blur-md transition-opacity duration-300 ${
            isListeningLive || isProcessing || isSpeakingReply ? 'opacity-85' : 'opacity-25'
          }`}
        />

        <form
          onSubmit={(e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            if (!typedInput.trim()) return;
            stopActiveListening();
            runAgentCommand(typedInput);
          }}
          className="relative flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-white/[0.08] border border-white/15 rounded-full px-2 py-1"
        >
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setShowQuickChips((v) => !v)}
            aria-label="Toggle suggested Astra commands"
            title="Quick command suggestions"
            className="w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-white/10 hover:bg-white/18 flex items-center justify-center text-slate-200 shrink-0"
          >
            <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          </button>

          <input
            ref={inputRef}
            type="text"
            value={typedInput}
            onChange={(e) => {
              setTypedInput(e.target.value);
              if (isListeningLive && e.target.value.length > 0) {
                stopActiveListening();
              }
            }}
            placeholder="Ask Astra (à°…à°¸à±à°¤à±à°°à°‚)..."
            aria-label="Ask Astra input"
            className="flex-1 bg-transparent text-xs text-white placeholder:text-slate-400 focus:outline-none px-1"
          />

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
              if (isListeningLive) stopActiveListening();
              else startStreamingListen();
            }}
            aria-label={isListeningLive ? 'Pause live microphone' : 'Start live microphone'}
            style={isListeningLive ? { backgroundColor: palette.primaryHex } : undefined}
            className={`w-7 h-7 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center transition-colors shrink-0 ${
              isListeningLive
                ? palette.onPrimaryTextClass
                : 'bg-white/10 hover:bg-white/18 text-slate-200'
            }`}
          >
            {isListeningLive ? (
              <Sparkles className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            ) : (
              <Mic className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            )}
          </button>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

  preferences: PillPreferences;
  onUpdatePreferences: (patch: Partial<PillPreferences>) => void;
  onBackToSimulator: () => void;
}

  | 'sliders_music'
  | 'control_designer'
  | 'island_geometry'
  | 'edge_sounds'
  | 'tabs_manager'
  | 'ai_astra';

}

export const FullSettingsView: React.FC<FullSettingsViewProps> = ({
  preferences,
  onUpdatePreferences,
  onBackToSimulator,
}) => {
  const [section, setSection] = useState<SettingsSectionId>('themes_icons');
  const [isSidebarCollapsed, setIsSidebarCollapsed] = useState(false);
  const [recordingShortcut, setRecordingShortcut] = useState(false);
  const [recordingAgentShortcut, setRecordingAgentShortcut] = useState(false);
  const [astraCategoryFilter, setAstraCategoryFilter] = useState<AstraCategory>('all');

  const [isFullscreen, setIsFullscreen] = useState(false);
  const [testBounce, setTestBounce] = useState(false);
  const [isRazorSlicing, setIsRazorSlicing] = useState(false);

  React.useEffect(() => {
    const onKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'F11') {
        e.preventDefault();
        handleToggleFullscreen();
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
  }, []);

  const handleMinimize = async () => {
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && '__TAURI_INTERNALS__' in window) {
      try {
        const { invoke } = await import('@tauri-apps/api/core');
        await invoke('minimize_settings_window');
        return;
      } catch {}
      try {
        const { getCurrentWebviewWindow } = await import('@tauri-apps/api/webviewWindow');
        await getCurrentWebviewWindow().minimize();
      } catch {}
    }
  };

  const handleMaximize = async () => {
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && '__TAURI_INTERNALS__' in window) {
      try {
        const { invoke } = await import('@tauri-apps/api/core');
        await invoke('toggle_maximize_settings_window');
        return;
      } catch {}
      try {
        const { getCurrentWebviewWindow } = await import('@tauri-apps/api/webviewWindow');
        await getCurrentWebviewWindow().toggleMaximize();
      } catch {}
    }
  };

  const handleToggleFullscreen = async () => {
    setIsFullscreen((prev) => !prev);
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && '__TAURI_INTERNALS__' in window) {
      try {
        const { invoke } = await import('@tauri-apps/api/core');
        await invoke('toggle_fullscreen_settings_window');
        return;
      } catch {}
      try {
        const { getCurrentWebviewWindow } = await import('@tauri-apps/api/webviewWindow');
        const win = getCurrentWebviewWindow();
        const cur = await win.isFullscreen();
        await win.setFullscreen(!cur);
      } catch {}
    }
  };

  const handleClose = async () => {
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && '__TAURI_INTERNALS__' in window) {
      try {
        const { invoke } = await import('@tauri-apps/api/core');
        await invoke('close_settings_window');
        return;
      } catch {}
      try {
        const { getCurrentWebviewWindow } = await import('@tauri-apps/api/webviewWindow');
        await getCurrentWebviewWindow().hide();
      } catch {}
    }
    onBackToSimulator();
  };

  // Interactive preview values so users can drag and test every slider & tile live inside Settings
  const [previewMusicPct, setPreviewMusicPct] = useState(64);
  const [previewVolumePct, setPreviewVolumePct] = useState(74);
  const [previewWifiOn, setPreviewWifiOn] = useState(true);
  const [previewBtOn, setPreviewBtOn] = useState(true);
  const [newCustomTabName, setNewCustomTabName] = useState('');

  const palette = resolveThemePalette(
    preferences.themeId,
    preferences.customPrimaryHex,
    preferences.customSecondaryHex
  );
  const iconMetrics = resolveIconPackMetrics(preferences.iconPack);
  const pillMetrics = resolveInnerPillMetrics(
    preferences.innerPillScale,
    preferences.innerCapsuleRadius
  );

  const recordKeyCombo = (
    e: React.KeyboardEvent,
    targetField: 'toggleShortcut' | 'voiceAgentShortcut',
    stopRecording: () => void
  ) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopPropagation();

    if (['Alt', 'Control', 'Shift', 'Meta'].includes(e.key)) return;
    if (e.key === 'Escape') {
      stopRecording();
      return;
    }

    const parts: string[] = [];
    if (e.ctrlKey) parts.push('Ctrl');
    if (e.altKey) parts.push('Alt');
    if (e.shiftKey) parts.push('Shift');
    if (parts.length === 0) parts.push('Alt');

    const keyLabel =
      e.code === 'Space'
        ? 'Space'
        : e.key.length === 1
          ? e.key.toUpperCase()
          : e.key;
    const combo = `${parts.join('+')}+${keyLabel}`;
    onUpdatePreferences({ [targetField]: combo });
    stopRecording();
    playTactilePop('confirm');
  };

  const toggleTabEnabled = (tab: BuiltinPillTab) => {
    const enabledCount = Object.values(preferences.enabledTabs).filter(Boolean).length;
    const currentlyOn = preferences.enabledTabs[tab];
    if (currentlyOn && enabledCount <= 1) return;
    onUpdatePreferences({
      enabledTabs: { ...preferences.enabledTabs, [tab]: !currentlyOn },
    });
    playTactilePop(!currentlyOn ? 'toggleOn' : 'toggleOff');
  };

  const handleAddCustomWidgetTab = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const label = newCustomTabName.trim() || `Deck ${(preferences.customWidgetTabs?.length ?? 0) + 1}`;
    const newTab: CustomWidgetTab = {
      id: `custom_${Date.now().toString(36)}`,
      name: label,
      enabled: true,
      widgets: ['timers', 'network_mini', 'vitals_mini', 'quick_actions'],
      widgetSizes: {
        timers: 'medium',
        network_mini: 'small',
        vitals_mini: 'small',
        quick_actions: 'medium',
      },
    };
    onUpdatePreferences({
      customWidgetTabs: [...(preferences.customWidgetTabs ?? []), newTab],
    });
    setNewCustomTabName('');
    playTactilePop('confirm');
  };

  const toggleFeatureFlag = (key: keyof PillFeatureFlags) => {
    const next = !preferences.featureFlags[key];
    onUpdatePreferences({
      featureFlags: { ...preferences.featureFlags, [key]: next },
    });
    playTactilePop(next ? 'toggleOn' : 'toggleOff');
  };

  const toggleControlTileIncluded = (id: ControlTileId) => {
    const exists = preferences.controlTiles.includes(id);
    const updated = exists
      ? preferences.controlTiles.filter((t) => t !== id)
      : [...preferences.controlTiles, id];
    if (updated.length > 0) {
      onUpdatePreferences({ controlTiles: updated });
      playTactilePop('tab');
    }
  };

  const toggleHomeWidgetIncluded = (id: HomeWidgetId) => {
    const exists = preferences.homeWidgets.includes(id);
    const updated = exists
      ? preferences.homeWidgets.filter((w) => w !== id)
      : [...preferences.homeWidgets, id];
    if (updated.length > 0) {
      onUpdatePreferences({ homeWidgets: updated });
      playTactilePop('tab');
    }
  };

  const NAV_ITEMS: {
    id: SettingsSectionId;
    label: string;
    Icon: React.ComponentType<{ className?: string; style?: React.CSSProperties }>;
  }[] = [
    { id: 'themes_icons', label: 'Color Themes, Icons & Pills', Icon: Palette },
    { id: 'sliders_music', label: 'Fused Sliders & Visualizers', Icon: Music },
    { id: 'control_designer', label: 'Control Center & > Menus', Icon: Grid },

                              : 'bg-white/[0.02] border-white/10 text-slate-300 hover:bg-white/[0.05]'
                          }`}

                          <div className="text-xs font-semibold">{sf.label}</div>

                            ? 'bg-white/[0.07]'
                            : 'bg-white/[0.02] border-white/10'
                        }`}
                      >
                        <div>
                          <div className="text-xs font-semibold text-white">{st.name}</div>

                          </div>

                          </button>
                          <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() => {
                              playTactilePop('tab');
                              onUpdatePreferences({ controlSliderStyle: st.id });
                            }}
                            style={isControl ? { backgroundColor: palette.primaryHex } : undefined}
                            className={`flex-1 py-1 px-2 rounded-lg text-[11px] font-medium transition-colors ${
                              isControl
                                ? `${palette.onPrimaryTextClass} font-semibold`
                                : 'bg-white/8 hover:bg-white/15 text-slate-300'
                            }`}
                          >
                            {isControl ? 'âœ“ Control Slider' : 'Use for Controls'}
                          </button>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    );
                  })}
                </div>

                <label className="block pt-2 border-t border-white/10">
                  <div className="flex justify-between text-xs text-slate-300 mb-1.5">

                        className={`p-3 rounded-xl border transition-colors cursor-pointer ${
                          active
                            ? 'bg-white/[0.08]'
                            : 'bg-white/[0.02] border-white/10 hover:bg-white/[0.05]'

                    [
                      {

                              type="button"
                              disabled={idx === preferences.controlTiles.length - 1}
                              aria-label={`Move ${tile.label} down`}

                                onUpdatePreferences({
                                  controlTiles: moveOrderedItem(
                                    preferences.controlTiles,
                                    idx,
                                    1
                                  ),

              {/* Smart Dynamic Clipboard Layout, Sort & Group Defaults */}

           * ============================================================== */}
          {section === 'island_geometry' && (

                  {(
                    [
                      {
                        id: PillShapeStyle.DynamicPill,
                        name: 'Dynamic Island Pill',

              </section>

              <section className="bg-white/[0.03] border border-white/10 rounded-2xl p-5 space-y-4">

                </h2>

                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-2">
                  {(
                    [
                      { id: PillAnchorPosition.TopCenter, label: 'Top Center' },

                      style={
                        preferences.anchorPosition === pos.id
                          ? { backgroundColor: palette.primaryHex }
                          : undefined
                      }
                      className={`py-2 px-3 rounded-xl border text-xs font-medium transition-colors ${
                        preferences.anchorPosition === pos.id
                          ? `${palette.onPrimaryTextClass} border-transparent font-semibold`
                          : 'bg-white/[0.03] border-white/10 text-slate-300 hover:text-white'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {pos.label}
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>

                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4 pt-2">
                  <div>
                    <div className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-300 mb-1.5">Material Finish</div>
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-1.5">
                      {(
                        [
                          { id: AppearanceMaterial.Obsidian, label: 'Obsidian' },
                          { id: AppearanceMaterial.LiquidGlass, label: 'Liquid Glass' },
                          { id: AppearanceMaterial.FrostedGraphite, label: 'Graphite' },
                        ] as const
                      ).map((m) => (
                        <button
                          key={m.id}
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => onUpdatePreferences({ material: m.id })}
                          style={
                            preferences.material === m.id
                              ? { borderColor: palette.primaryHex }
                              : undefined
                          }
                          className={`py-1.5 px-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-medium border transition-colors ${
                            preferences.material === m.id
                              ? 'bg-white/12 text-white'
                              : 'bg-white/[0.03] border-white/10 text-slate-400 hover:text-white'
                          }`}
                        >
                          {m.label}
                        </button>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <label className="block">
                    <div className="flex justify-between text-xs text-slate-300 mb-1.5">
                      <span>Floating Edge Margin</span>
                      <span className="font-mono tabular-nums" style={{ color: palette.primaryHex }}>
                        {preferences.floatingOffsetPx} px
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    <input
                      type="range"
                      min={0}
                      max={28}
                      value={preferences.floatingOffsetPx}
                      onChange={(e) =>
                        onUpdatePreferences({ floatingOffsetPx: Number(e.target.value) })
                      }
                      className="w-full h-1.5 accent-cyan-400 bg-white/15 rounded-full cursor-pointer"
                    />
                  </label>
                </div>

                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 pt-2 border-t border-white/10">
                  <span className="text-xs text-slate-400 mr-1">Outer Scale:</span>
                  {(
                    [
                      { id: PillSizeScale.Small, label: 'Small' },
                      { id: PillSizeScale.Medium, label: 'Medium' },
                      { id: PillSizeScale.Large, label: 'Large' },
                    ] as const
                  ).map((sz) => (
                    <button
                      key={sz.id}
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => onUpdatePreferences({ sizeScale: sz.id })}
                      className={`px-3 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-medium ${
                        preferences.sizeScale === sz.id
                          ? 'bg-white/20 text-white'
                          : 'bg-white/[0.04] text-slate-400 hover:text-white'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {sz.label}
                    </button>
                  ))}

                  <span className="text-xs text-slate-400 ml-3 mr-1">Spring:</span>
                  {(
                    [
                      { id: SpringPresetId.LiquidSilk, label: 'Liquid Silk' },
                      { id: SpringPresetId.ElasticDrop, label: 'Elastic' },
                      { id: SpringPresetId.Snappy, label: 'Snappy' },
                      { id: SpringPresetId.Calm, label: 'Calm' },
                    ] as const
                  ).map((sp) => (
                    <button
                      key={sp.id}
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() =>
                        onUpdatePreferences({
                          springPreset: sp.id,
                          customSpring: { ...SPRING_PRESETS[sp.id] },
                        })
                      }
                      className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-medium ${
                        preferences.springPreset === sp.id
                          ? 'bg-white/20 text-white'
                          : 'bg-white/[0.04] text-slate-400 hover:text-white'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {sp.label}
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </section>

              {/* Stealth Edge-Touch Hide */}
              <section className="bg-white/[0.03] border border-white/10 rounded-2xl p-5 space-y-4">
                <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4">
                  <div>
                    <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold text-white flex items-center gap-2">
                      <EyeOff className="w-4 h-4" style={{ color: palette.primaryHex }} />
                      <span>Stealth Auto-Hide (Screen-Edge Touch or Shortcut Reveal)</span>
                    </h2>
                    <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
                      Hides the pill completely off-screen until your cursor touches the monitor edge or you press your global shortcut.
                    </p>
                  </div>
                  <input
                    type="checkbox"
                    aria-label="Hide Pill until edge touch or keyboard shortcut"
                    checked={preferences.stealthAutoHide}
                    onChange={(e) => {
                      playTactilePop(e.target.checked ? 'toggleOn' : 'toggleOff');
                      onUpdatePreferences({ stealthAutoHide: e.target.checked });
                    }}
                    className="w-5 h-5 rounded accent-cyan-400 shrink-0 mt-0.5"
                  />
                </div>

                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3 pt-2 border-t border-white/10">
                  <label className="flex items-center justify-between p-3 rounded-xl bg-white/[0.03] border border-white/10 cursor-pointer">
                    <div>
                      <div className="text-xs font-medium text-white">Reveal on Screen-Edge Touch</div>
                      <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">
                        Glide cursor to top/bottom bezel to summon
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <input
                      type="checkbox"
                      checked={preferences.revealOnEdgeTouch}
                      onChange={(e) =>
                        onUpdatePreferences({ revealOnEdgeTouch: e.target.checked })
                      }
                      className="w-4 h-4 rounded accent-cyan-400 shrink-0 ml-2"
                    />
                  </label>

                  <label className="flex items-center justify-between p-3 rounded-xl bg-white/[0.03] border border-white/10 cursor-pointer">
                    <div>
                      <div className="text-xs font-medium text-white">Auto-Close Settings on Mouse Leave</div>
                      <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">
                        Dismiss settings window automatically when cursor exits
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <input
                      type="checkbox"
                      checked={preferences.closeSettingsOnMouseLeave ?? true}
                      onChange={(e) =>
                        onUpdatePreferences({ closeSettingsOnMouseLeave: e.target.checked })
                      }
                      className="w-4 h-4 rounded accent-cyan-400 shrink-0 ml-2"
                    />
                  </label>

                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-3 rounded-xl bg-white/[0.03] border border-white/10">
                    <div>
                      <div className="text-xs font-medium text-white flex items-center gap-1.5">
                        <Keyboard className="w-3.5 h-3.5" style={{ color: palette.primaryHex }} />
                        <span>Island Summon Shortcut</span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">Expand / collapse pill</div>
                    </div>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onKeyDown={(e) =>
                        recordingShortcut &&
                        recordKeyCombo(e, 'toggleShortcut', () => setRecordingShortcut(false))
                      }
                      onClick={() => {
                        setRecordingAgentShortcut(false);
                        setRecordingShortcut((v) => !v);
                      }}
                      className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-mono font-semibold border transition-colors ${
                        recordingShortcut
                          ? 'bg-amber-500/25 border-amber-400 text-amber-200'
                          : 'bg-black/50 border-white/15 text-white hover:border-white/40'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {recordingShortcut ? 'Press keys...' : preferences.toggleShortcut}
                    </button>
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-3 rounded-xl bg-white/[0.03] border border-white/10">
                    <div>
                      <div className="text-xs font-medium text-white flex items-center gap-1.5">
                        <Mic className="w-3.5 h-3.5" style={{ color: palette.primaryHex }} />
                        <span>Windows Voice Agent Shortcut</span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">
                        Siri / Assistant toggle for Wi-Fi, Volume &amp; Search
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onKeyDown={(e) =>
                        recordingAgentShortcut &&
                        recordKeyCombo(e, 'voiceAgentShortcut', () =>
                          setRecordingAgentShortcut(false)
                        )
                      }
                      onClick={() => {
                        setRecordingShortcut(false);
                        setRecordingAgentShortcut((v) => !v);
                      }}
                      className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-mono font-semibold border transition-colors ${
                        recordingAgentShortcut
                          ? 'bg-amber-500/25 border-amber-400 text-amber-200'
                          : 'bg-black/50 border-white/15 text-white hover:border-white/40'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {recordingAgentShortcut ? 'Press keys...' : preferences.voiceAgentShortcut}
                    </button>
                  </div>

                  <label className="flex items-center justify-between p-3 rounded-xl bg-white/[0.03] border border-white/10 cursor-pointer">
                    <div>
                      <div className="text-xs font-medium text-white">
                        Voice Agent Spoken Replies
                      </div>
                      <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">
                        Reads assistant confirmations aloud via Gemini TTS
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <input
                      type="checkbox"
                      checked={preferences.voiceAgentSpeakReplies}
                      onChange={(e) =>
                        onUpdatePreferences({ voiceAgentSpeakReplies: e.target.checked })
                      }
                      className="w-4 h-4 rounded accent-cyan-400"
                    />
                  </label>
                </div>

                {/* Cursor Reveal Area Geometry & Bezel Radar (Configurable Length & Height Limits) */}
                <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-white/[0.02] border border-white/10 space-y-4">
                  <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3">
                    <div>
                      <div className="text-xs font-semibold text-white flex items-center gap-2">
                        <Sliders className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-cyan-400" />
                        <span>Screen-Edge Reveal Trigger Zone (Cursor Hit Bounds)</span>
                      </div>
                      <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
                        Controls the horizontal length and edge depth required to reveal the Pill. Can be tuned from 1px up to 600px, or completely disabled.
                      </p>
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
                      <span
                        className={`text-[11px] font-mono px-2 py-0.5 rounded border ${
                          preferences.revealOnEdgeTouch
                            ? 'bg-cyan-500/10 text-cyan-300 border-cyan-500/20'
                            : 'bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-300 border-rose-500/20'
                        }`}
                      >
                        {preferences.revealOnEdgeTouch
                          ? `${preferences.revealAreaWidth ?? 140}px Ã— ${preferences.revealAreaHeight ?? 4}px`
                          : 'Disabled'}
                      </span>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => {
                          playTactilePop(preferences.revealOnEdgeTouch ? 'toggleOff' : 'toggleOn');
                          onUpdatePreferences({ revealOnEdgeTouch: !preferences.revealOnEdgeTouch });
                        }}
                        className={`px-2.5 py-1 text-xs rounded-lg font-medium border transition-colors ${
                          preferences.revealOnEdgeTouch
                            ? 'bg-rose-500/15 border-rose-500/30 text-rose-300 hover:bg-rose-500/25'
                            : 'bg-emerald-500/15 border-emerald-500/30 text-emerald-300 hover:bg-emerald-500/25'
                        }`}
                      >
                        {preferences.revealOnEdgeTouch ? 'Completely Disable' : 'Enable Sensor'}
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* If Completely Disabled Banner */}
                  {!preferences.revealOnEdgeTouch ? (
                    <div className="p-3.5 rounded-xl bg-rose-500/10 border border-rose-500/25 flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-start sm:items-center justify-between gap-3">
                      <div className="space-y-0.5">
                        <div className="text-xs font-semibold text-rose-200 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                          <span>ðŸ›¡ï¸ Cursor Edge Auto-Reveal is Completely Disabled</span>
                        </div>
                        <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-300 leading-relaxed">
                          Moving your cursor to the top screen edge will <strong>never</strong> trigger or drop down the Pill. Use <strong>Alt+K</strong> to drop the compact pill from top, or <strong>Alt+L</strong> to expand full studio.
                        </p>
                      </div>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => {
                          playTactilePop('toggleOn');
                          onUpdatePreferences({ revealOnEdgeTouch: true });
                        }}
                        className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-cyan-500 text-slate-950 font-semibold text-xs hover:bg-cyan-400 transition-colors shrink-0"
                      >
                        Re-enable Sensor
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  ) : (
                    <>
                      {/* Interactive Monitor Bezel Radar Preview */}
                      <div className="relative h-20 w-full rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-b from-[#0c0f17] to-[#07090e] border border-white/10 overflow-hidden flex flex-col items-center">
                        <div className="w-full h-3 bg-slate-900/90 border-b border-white/10 flex items-center justify-center">
                          <span className="text-[9px] font-mono text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-widest">Top Monitor Bezel</span>
                        </div>
                        <div className="flex-1 w-full relative flex items-start justify-center">
                          {/* Dynamic Radar Trigger Zone */}
                          <div
                            style={{
                              width: `${Math.min(100, Math.max(1, ((preferences.revealAreaWidth ?? 140) / 600) * 100))}%`,
                              height: `${Math.min(32, Math.max(4, (preferences.revealAreaHeight ?? 4) * 2.5))}px`,
                              borderColor: palette.primaryHex,
                              boxShadow: `0 0 16px ${palette.primaryHex}50`,
                            }}
                            className="border-b-2 border-x-2 rounded-b-lg bg-cyan-500/15 flex items-center justify-center transition-all duration-150"
                          >
                            <span className="text-[9px] font-mono font-medium text-cyan-200">
                              {preferences.revealAreaWidth ?? 140}px Zone
                            </span>
                          </div>
                          <div className="absolute top-1 right-3 text-[10px] text-slate-500 font-mono">
                            Height Limit: {preferences.revealAreaHeight ?? 4}px
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      </div>

                      {/* Dual Sliders: Length Limit (down to 1px) & Height Limit */}
                      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                        {/* Length / Width Limit Slider (down to 1px) */}
                        <div className="space-y-2 p-3 rounded-xl bg-black/20 border border-white/5">
                          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                            <label className="text-xs font-medium text-white flex items-center gap-1.5">
                              <span>Reveal Trigger Length Limit</span>
                            </label>
                            <span className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-cyan-400">
                              {preferences.revealAreaWidth ?? 140}px
                            </span>
                          </div>
                          <input
                            type="range"
                            min={1}
                            max={600}
                            step={1}
                            value={preferences.revealAreaWidth ?? 140}
                            onChange={(e) => {
                              const val = Math.max(1, Number(e.target.value));
                              onUpdatePreferences({ revealAreaWidth: val });
                            }}
                            className="w-full accent-cyan-400 cursor-pointer"
                          />
                          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-1 pt-1">
                            {[
                              { label: 'Pinpoint (1px)', val: 1 },
                              { label: 'Micro (40px)', val: 40 },
                              { label: 'Ultra (80px)', val: 80 },
                              { label: 'Compact (140px)', val: 140 },
                              { label: 'Medium (240px)', val: 240 },
                              { label: 'Wide (380px)', val: 380 },
                            ].map((preset) => (
                              <button
                                key={preset.val}
                                type="button"
                                onClick={() => onUpdatePreferences({ revealAreaWidth: preset.val })}
                                className={`px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] rounded-lg border font-mono transition-all ${
                                  (preferences.revealAreaWidth ?? 140) === preset.val
                                    ? 'bg-cyan-500/20 border-cyan-400 text-cyan-200 font-semibold'
                                    : 'bg-white/5 border-white/10 text-slate-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-white/10'
                                }`}
                              >
                                {preset.label}
                              </button>
                            ))}
                          </div>
                        </div>

                        {/* Height Limit (Edge Distance) Slider */}
                        <div className="space-y-2 p-3 rounded-xl bg-black/20 border border-white/5">
                          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                            <label className="text-xs font-medium text-white flex items-center gap-1.5">
                              <span>Edge Depth / Height Limit</span>
                            </label>
                            <span className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-cyan-400">
                              {preferences.revealAreaHeight ?? 4}px
                            </span>
                          </div>
                          <input
                            type="range"
                            min={1}
                            max={20}
                            step={1}
                            value={preferences.revealAreaHeight ?? 4}
                            onChange={(e) => {
                              const val = Math.max(1, Number(e.target.value));
                              onUpdatePreferences({ revealAreaHeight: val });
                            }}
                            className="w-full accent-cyan-400 cursor-pointer"
                          />
                          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-1 pt-1">
                            {[
                              { label: 'Razor (1px)', val: 1 },
                              { label: 'Hairline (2px)', val: 2 },
                              { label: 'Subtle (4px)', val: 4 },
                              { label: 'Standard (8px)', val: 8 },
                              { label: 'Deep (14px)', val: 14 },
                            ].map((preset) => (
                              <button
                                key={preset.val}
                                type="button"
                                onClick={() => onUpdatePreferences({ revealAreaHeight: preset.val })}
                                className={`px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] rounded-lg border font-mono transition-all ${
                                  (preferences.revealAreaHeight ?? 4) === preset.val
                                    ? 'bg-cyan-500/20 border-cyan-400 text-cyan-200 font-semibold'
                                    : 'bg-white/5 border-white/10 text-slate-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-white/10'
                                }`}
                              >
                                {preset.label}
                              </button>
                            ))}
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </>
                  )}

                  {/* Dual Keyboard Shortcuts Guide */}
                  <div className="pt-2 border-t border-white/5 flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-2 text-[11px] text-slate-400">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      <kbd className="px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-white/10 font-mono text-cyan-300 border border-white/15">Alt + K</kbd>
                      <span>Reveal compact pill from top (2nd method)</span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      <kbd className="px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-white/10 font-mono text-cyan-300 border border-white/15">Alt + L</kbd>
                      <span>Expand / collapse full studio</span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </section>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* ================================================================
           * SECTION 5: EDGE LIGHTING & PLEASANT ACOUSTIC SOUNDSCAPES
           * ============================================================== */}
          {section === 'edge_sounds' && (
            <div className="space-y-6">
              <section className="bg-white/[0.03] border border-white/10 rounded-2xl p-5 space-y-4">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                  <div>
                    <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold text-white flex items-center gap-2">
                      <Sparkles className="w-4 h-4" style={{ color: palette.primaryHex }} />
                      <span>Perimeter Edge Lighting Profiles</span>
                    </h2>
                    <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
                      Optical rim glow around the Dynamic Island silhouette.
                    </p>
                  </div>
                  <input
                    type="checkbox"
                    checked={preferences.featureFlags.edgeLighting}
                    onChange={() => toggleFeatureFlag('edgeLighting')}
                    className="w-5 h-5 rounded accent-cyan-400"
                  />
                </div>

                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-2.5">
                  {(
                    [
                      { id: 'album_adaptive', name: 'Theme / Album Adaptive', preview: 'from-cyan-400 via-sky-400 to-indigo-500' },
                      { id: 'apple_aura', name: 'Iridescent Fluid Aura', preview: 'from-sky-400 via-fuchsia-400 to-amber-300' },
                      { id: 'northern_aurora', name: 'Northern Aurora', preview: 'from-emerald-400 via-teal-400 to-cyan-400' },
                      { id: 'solar_amber', name: 'Solar Tungsten Gold', preview: 'from-amber-300 via-orange-400 to-rose-400' },
                      { id: 'glacial_ice', name: 'Glacial Silver-Blue', preview: 'from-slate-200 via-sky-300 to-blue-500' },
                      { id: 'crimson_pulse', name: 'Deep Ruby Focus', preview: 'from-rose-400 via-red-500 to-orange-500' },
                      { id: 'off', name: 'No Edge Glow', preview: 'from-slate-800 to-slate-900' },
                    ] as { id: EdgeLightProfile; name: string; preview: string }[]
                  ).map((prof) => {
                    const selected = preferences.edgeLightProfile === prof.id;
                    return (
                      <button
                        key={prof.id}
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => {
                          playTactilePop('tab');
                          onUpdatePreferences({
                            edgeLightProfile: prof.id,
                            featureFlags: {
                              ...preferences.featureFlags,
                              edgeLighting: prof.id !== 'off',
                            },
                          });
                        }}
                        style={selected ? { borderColor: palette.primaryHex } : undefined}
                        className={`flex items-center justify-between p-3 rounded-xl border text-left transition-colors ${
                          selected
                            ? 'bg-white/10 text-white'
                            : 'bg-white/[0.02] border-white/10 text-slate-300 hover:bg-white/[0.05]'
                        }`}
                      >
                        <span className="text-xs font-semibold">{prof.name}</span>
                        <span className={`w-14 h-3.5 rounded-full bg-gradient-to-r ${prof.preview}`} />
                      </button>
                    );
                  })}
                </div>
              </section>

              <section className="bg-white/[0.03] border border-white/10 rounded-2xl p-5 space-y-4">
                <div>
                  <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold text-white">
                    Pleasant Acoustic Soundscape Profiles
                  </h2>
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
                    Gentle low-pass-filtered acoustic articulations with distinct notes per action.
                  </p>
                </div>

                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-2.5">
                  {(
                    [
                      { id: 'wooden_marimba', name: 'Warm Rosewood Marimba', desc: 'Soft muted woodblock harmonics (Default)' },
                      { id: 'velvet_droplet', name: 'Velvet Water Droplet', desc: 'Deep liquid meniscus ripple' },
                      { id: 'brushed_glass', name: 'Brushed Crystal Fifths', desc: 'Airy musical fifth interval' },
                      { id: 'taptic_thump', name: 'Taptic Micro-Impulse', desc: 'Sub-bass physical thump' },
                      { id: 'cyber_synth', name: 'Cyberpunk Neon Synth', desc: 'Crisp sci-fi FM harmonic blip' },
                      { id: 'retro_cassette', name: 'Retro Cassette Latch', desc: 'Warm 80s analog tape-deck click' },
                      { id: 'mechanical_gt', name: 'Motorsport GT Paddle', desc: 'Precision carbon paddle-shift detent' },
                      { id: 'zen_garden', name: 'Botanical & Paper Chime', desc: 'Soft bamboo & sumi-ink bell harmonic' },
                      { id: 'naruto_shinobi', name: 'Shinobi Jutsu Acoustic', desc: 'Rasengan chakra spin whistle, Chidori snap, Sharingan ocular chime' },
                      { id: 'silent', name: 'Silent (Muted)', desc: 'Disables all sound effects' },
                    ] as { id: SoundProfileId; name: string; desc: string }[]
                  ).map((snd) => {
                    const selected = preferences.soundProfile === snd.id;
                    return (
                      <button
                        key={snd.id}
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => {
                          onUpdatePreferences({ soundProfile: snd.id });
                          if (snd.id !== 'silent') playTactilePop('expand', snd.id);
                        }}
                        style={selected ? { borderColor: palette.primaryHex } : undefined}
                        className={`p-3 rounded-xl border text-left transition-colors ${
                          selected
                            ? 'bg-white/10 text-white'
                            : 'bg-white/[0.02] border-white/10 text-slate-300 hover:bg-white/[0.05]'
